                     pattuppāṭṭil āṟāvatāṉa
                              maturaik kāñci

       Source:
       pattuppāṭṭu mūlamum maturaiyāciriyar pārattuvāci
       nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruraiyum.
       ivai makāmakōpāttiyāya tākṣiṇātya kalāniti uttamatāṉapuram, vē.
       cāminātaiyarāl paricōtittu, palavakai ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ
       ceṉṉai : kēcari accukkūṭattiṟ patippikkappeṟṟaṉa.
       (mūṉṟām patippu) pirajōtpatti varuṭam āvaṇi mātam.
       Copyright Registered] - 1931 [vilai rūpā. 5

                          maturaik kāñci -mūlam

   ōṅkutirai viyaṉparappi
       ṉolimunnīr varampākat
       tēṉṟūṅku muyarcimaiya
       malaināṟiya viyaṉñālattu
       valamātirattāṉ vaḷikoṭpa         5
       viyaṉāṇmī ṉeṟiyoḻukap
       pakaṟceyyuñ ceññāyiṟu
       miravucceyyum veṇṭiṅkaḷu
       maitīrntu kiḷarntuviḷaṅka
       maḻaitoḻi lutava mātiraṅ koḻukkat         10
       toṭuppi ṉāyiram vittiyatu viḷaiya
       nilaṉu maraṉum payaṉetirpu nanta
       nōyikantu nōkkuviḷaṅka
       mētaka mikappolinta
       vōṅkunilai vayakkaḷiṟu         15
       kaṇṭutaṇṭāk kaṭkiṉpat
       tuṇṭutaṇṭā mikuvaḷattā
       ṉuyarpūrima viḻutteruviṟ
       poyyaṟiyā vāymoḻiyāṟ
       pukaḻniṟainta naṉmāntaroṭu         20
       nallūḻi yaṭippaṭarap
       palveḷḷa mīkkūṟa
       vulaka māṇṭa vuyarntōr maruka
       piṇakkōṭṭa kaḷiṟṟukkuḻumpi
       ṉiṇamvāyppeyta pēymakaḷi         25
       riṇaiyoliyimiḻ tuṇaṅkaiccīrp
       piṇaiyūpa meḻuntāṭa
       vañcuvanta pōrkkaḷattā
       ṉāṇṭalai yaṇaṅkaṭuppiṉ
       vayavēnta roṇkuruti         30
       ciṉattīyiṟ peyarpupoṅkat
       teṟalaruṅ kaṭuntuppiṉ
       viṟalviḷaṅkiya viḻuccūrppiṟ
       ṟoṭittōṭkai tuṭuppāka
       vāṭuṟṟa vūṉcōṟu         35
       neṟiyaṟinta kaṭivāluva
       ṉaṭiyotuṅkip piṟpeyarāp
       paṭaiyōrkku murukayara
       vamarkaṭakkum viyaṉṟāṉait
       teṉṉavaṟ peyariya tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ         40
       ṟoṉmutu kaṭavuṭ piṉṉar mēya
       varaittā ḻaruvip poruppiṟ poruna
       viḻuccūḻiya viḷaṅkōṭaiya
       kaṭuñciṉatta kamaḻkaṭāattaḷaṟupaṭṭa naṟuñceṉṉiya         45
       varaimaruḷu muyartōṉṟala
       viṉainaviṉṟa pēryāṉai
       ciṉañciṟantu kaḷaṉuḻakkavu
       māveṭutta malikurūuttuka
       ḷakalvāṉattu veyilkarappavum         50
       vāmpariya kaṭuntiṇṭēr
       kāṟṟeṉṉak kaṭitukoṭpavum
       vāṇmiku maṟamaintar
       tōṇmuṟaiyāṉ vīṟumuṟṟavu
       miruperu vēntaroṭu vēḷir cāyap         55
       porutavaraic ceruveṉṟu
       milaṅkaruviya varai nīntic
       curampōḻnta vikalāṟṟa
       luyarntōṅkiya viḻucciṟappi
       ṉilantanta pērutavip         60
       polantār mārpi ṉeṭiyō ṉumpaṉ
       marantiṉṉūu varaiyutirkku
       naraiyurumi ṉēṟaṉaiyai
       yaruṅkuḻumiḷaik kuṇṭukiṭaṅki
       ṉuyarntōṅkiya niraipputavi         65
       ṉeṭumati ṉiraiñāyi
       lampumi ḻayilaruppan
       taṇṭātu talaicceṉṟu
       koṇṭunīṅkiya viḻucciṟappiṟ
       ṟeṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkal         70
       kuṇakuṭakaṭa lāvellait
       toṉṟumoḻintu toḻilkēṭpa
       veṟṟamoṭu veṟuttoḻukiya
       koṟṟavartaṅ kōṉākuvai
       vāṉiyainta virumunnīrp         75
       pēenilaiiya virumpauvattuk
       koṭumpuṇari vilaṅkupōḻak
       kaṭuṅkāloṭu karaicēra
       neṭuṅkoṭimicai yitaiyeṭut
       tiṉṉicaiya muracamuḻuṅkap         80
       poṉmalinta viḻuppaṇṭa
       nāṭāra naṉkiḻitaru
       māṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
       maḻaimuṟṟiya malaipuraiyat
       tuṟaimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkait         85
       teṇkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
       cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelli
       ṉūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava
       nīrttevvu niraittoḻuvar
       pāṭucilampu micaiyēṟṟat         90
       tōṭuvaḻaṅku makalāmpiyiṟ
       kayaṉakaiya vayaṉiṟaikku
       meṉṟoṭai vaṉkiḻā a
       ratari koḷpavar pakaṭupūṇ ṭeṇmaṇi
       yirumpu ḷōppu micaiyē yeṉṟu         95
       maṇippū muṇṭakattu maṇaṉmali kāṉaṟ
       paratavar makaḷir kuravaiyo ṭolippa
       orucār, viḻavuniṉṟa viyalāṅkaṇ

   --------
   @vīṭu pēṟu nimittamākac cāṉṟōr palvēṟu nilaiyāmaiyai aṟainta
   maturaikkāñci kāñcittiṇaikku utāraṇameṉpar; tol. puṟat. cū. 23, na.
   1. nērpuniraiyākiya āciriyavuriccīr vañciyuḷ vantataṟkum (tol. cey. cū.
   14, pēr.), kuṟaḷaṭikkum (tol. cey. cū. 40. na.) ivvaṭi mēṟkōḷ.
   1-2. vaḻimōṉaikku ivvaṭikaḷ mēṟkōḷ ; tol. cey. cū. 94, pēr.
   3. (pi-m.) 'tūṅkiyavuyar'
   tēṉ tūṅkumuyarcimaiyam : "piracan tūṅku malaikiḻavōṟkē" (kuṟun. 392:8)
   ; "piracan tūṅku cēṭcimai, varai" (akanā. 242 : 21-2)
   4. "māmalai ñāṟiya ñālam" (pari. "vāṉāreḻili") ; "kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk
   kālattē" (pu. ve. 35)
   3-4. ivvaṭikaḷ patiṉeṭṭeḻuttāṉ vantaṉaveṉpar ; tol. cey. cū. 50, iḷam.
   1-4. yā. vi. cey. cū. 2, mēṟ.
   5. "vaḷivalaṅ koṭku mātiram vaḷampaṭum" (maṇi. 12 : 91)
   cintaṭiyeṉpataṟku ivvaṭi mēṟkōḷ ; tol. cey. cū. 38, na.
   7-8. perumpāṇ. 442-iṉ uraiyaiyum kuṟippuraiyaiyum pārkka.
   muraṇiraṉiṟaikku ivvaṭikaḷ mēṟkōḷ ; yā. vi. cū. 95, urai
   6-10. "mīṉvayi ṉiṟpa vāṉam vāyppa" (patiṟ. 90: 1)
   11. toṭuppu : "toṭuppē ruḻava rōtaip pāṇiyum" (cilap. 27: 230)
   āyiram : "vēli yāyiram viḷaiyuṭ ṭāka" (poruna. 246-7)
   10-11. "vāṉa miṉṉuvacivu poḻiya vāṉā, tiṭṭa vellām peṭṭāṅku viḷaiya"
   (malaipaṭu. 97-8)
   12. (pi-m.) 'etirpunta'
   14. (pi-m.) 'mētakap polinta'
   17. (pi-m.) 'taṇṭāviku'
   16-7. āṟeḻuttu irucīraṭi vañcippāviṟku ivvaṭikaḷ mēṟkōḷ ; yā. vi. cū.
   95, urai.
   18. (pi-m.) 'pūriya viḻutteru'
   21. "nallūḻi yāvarkkum piḻaiyātu varutaṉiṉ" (kalit. 99:5-6) ; "muta
   lūḻiyiṉpam, varavip paṭitaṉṉai vāḻvitta vāṇaṉ" (tañcai. 286) ; "vētamu
   meymmaiyu mātiyukampōlat talaiciṟappa vantaruḷi" (vikrama. meyk.
   "pūmālai") ; "ātiyukaṅ koḻuntu viṭṭut taḻaittōṅka" (iraṇṭām irācairāca.
   meyk. "pūmaruviya poḻil")
   22. "āyira veḷḷam vāḻiya palavē" (patiṟ. 21:38)
   24. piṇakkōṭṭa kaḷiṟu : "vēlāṇ mukatta kaḷiṟu" (kuṟaḷ, 500)
   25. (pi-m.) 'pēey'
   26. tuṇaṅkai : perumpāṇ. 235-ām aṭiyiṉ kuṟippuraiyaip pārkka.
   25-6. "niṇantiṉ vāya ṭuṇaṅkai tūṅka" (muruku. 56) eṉpataiyum ataṉ
   kuṟippuraiyaiyum pārkka.
   28. (pi-m.) 'vannta'
   34. "tōḷuṅ koṇṭu tuṭuppeṉat tuḻāvik koḷḷīr" (takka. 748)
   34-5. "piṭittāṭi yaṉṉa piṟaḻpaṟpē yārak, koṭittāṉai maṉṉaṉ koṭuttāṉ -
   muṭittalait, tōḷoṭu vīḻnta toṭikkai tuṭuppāka, mūḷaiyañ cōṟṟai mukantu"
   (pu. ve. 160)
   34-6. takka. 748, urai, mēṟ.
   29-35. "muṭittalai yaṭuppākap, puṉaṟkuruti yulaikkoḷīit, toṭittōṭ
   ṭuṭuppiṟ ṟuḻanta valciyi, ṉaṭukaḷam vēṭṭa vaṭupōrc ceḻiya" (puṟanā.
   26:8-11)
   37. "piṟakkaṭi yotuṅkāp pūṭkai" (patiṟ. 80:8)
   38. (pi-m.) 'paṭaiyōr murukayara'
   29-38. "muṭittalai yaṭuppiṟ piṭarttalait tāḻit, toṭit tōṭṭuṭuppiṟ
   ṟuḻaiiya vūṉcōṟu, maṟappēy vāluvaṉ vayiṉaṟin tūṭṭa" (cilap. 26:242-4)
   40-42. "maṟaimutu mutalvaṉ piṉṉar mēya, poṟaiyuyar potiyiṟ poruppaṉ"
   (cilap. 12 : iṟutippakuti)
   44. yāṉaimatam kamaḻtal : "kutipāy kaṭām, matakōṭi yula kēḻu maṇanāṟa"
   (takka. 3) eṉpataṉ vicēṭakkuṟippaip pārkka ; "māva tattiṉai yiḻaittiṭum
   pūṭkaiyiṉ matanīr, kāva tattiṉuṅ kamaḻtaru kaliṅkanāṭu" (kanta.
   mārkkaṇṭēyap. 114)
   45. (pi-m.) 'ayaṟupaṭṭanaṟuñ'; puṟanā. 22:7
   46. perumpāṇ. 352 ; puṟanā. 38:1, 42:1.
   47. "toḻiṉavilyāṉai" (patiṟ. 84:4)
   44-7. kaṭuñciṉatta yāṉai: "kaṭuñciṉatta kaḷiṟu" (maturaik. 179);
   "kaṭuñciṉatta kaḷiṟṟeruttiṉ" (yā. vi. cū. 56, mēṟ. "tāḻirum")
   48. (pi-m.) 'uḻakka'
   44-8. "celcaman tolaitta viṉainavil yāṉai, kaṭāam vārntu kaṭuñciṉam
   potti, vaṇṭupaṭu ceṉṉiya piṭipuṇarntiyala" (patiṟ. 82:4-6)
   50. (pi-m.) 'veyiṟkarappa'
   51. (pi-m.) 'vāppariya'
   52. (pi-m.) 'kāṟṟeṉak'
   51-2. "kāleṉak kaṭukkuṅ kaviṉpeṟu tērum" (maturaik. 388)
   55-6. "koycuvaṟ puravik koṭittērc ceḻiya, ṉālaṅkāṉat takaṉṟalai
   civappac, cēral cempiyaṉ ciṉaṅkeḻu titiyaṉ, pōrval liyāṉaip polampū
   ṇeḻiṉi, nāraṟi naṟavi ṉerumai yūraṉ, tēṅkama ḻalakattup pularnta cānti,
   ṉiruṅkō vēṇmā ṉiyaṟērp porunaṉeṉ, ṟeḻuvar nalvala maṭaṅka vorupakal,
   muraicoṭu veṇkuṭai yakappaṭut turaicelak, koṉṟu", (akanā. 36:13-22) ;
   "eḻuvar nalvalaṅ kaṭantōy", "puṉaikaḻa leḻuvar nalvala maṭaṅka, vorutā
   ṉākip porutukaḷat taṭalē" (puṟanā. 19 : 17, 76:12-3)
   58. (pi-m.) 'malainīnti'
   ilaṅkaruviya varai : "ilaṅku maruvittē yilaṅku maruvittē, vāṉi ṉilaṅku
   maruvittē tāṉuṟṟa, cūḷpēṇāṉ poyttāṉ mālai" (kalit. 41:18-20)
   61. tārmārpu : "vaṇṇa mārpiṟṟār" (puṟanā. 1:2) ; neṭiyōṉ : "munnīr
   viḻavi ṉeṭiyōṉ" (puṟanā. 9:10) ; "uraicāl ciṟappi ṉeṭiyōṉ" (cilap.
   22:60)
   60 - 61. nilantanta pērutavi neṭiyōṉ : "nilantaru tiruvi ṉeṭiyōṉ pōla"
   (maturaik. 763) ; "nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ" (tol. pāyiram)
   63. "arunarai yurumiṟ porunaraip poṟāac, cerumāṇ pañcava rēṟē" (puṟanā.
   58:7-8)
   67. "ampumiḻva vēlumiḻva" (cīvaka. 103)
   64 - 7. "kaṭimiḷaik kuṇṭukiṭaṅki, ṉeṭumati ṉiraiñāyi, lampuṭai yāreyil"
   (patiṟ. 20:17-9)
   70 - 71. mu. puṟanā. 17:1-2.
   72. mu. o. maturaik. 124 ; patiṟ. 90:8.
   70 - 72. "vaṭāatu paṉipaṭu neṭuvarai vaṭakkum, teṉāa turukeḻu kumariyiṉ
   ṟeṟkum, kuṇāatu karaiporu toṭukaṭaṟ kuṇakkum, kuṭāatu toṉṟumutir
   pauvattiṉ kuṭakkum", "teṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkaṟ, kuṇakuṭakaṭa lāvellai,
   kuṉṟumalai kāṭunā, ṭoṉṟupaṭṭu vaḻimoḻiya" (puṟanā. 6:1-4, 17:1-4)
   80. kappalil muracam muḻaṅkutal : "āṭukoṭi yucciyaṇi kūmpiṉuyar pāymūṉ,
   ṟīṭupaṭac ceytiḷaiya rēttavimiḻ munnīrk, kōṭu paṟai yārppa
   ................ ōṭiyatai yaṉṟē" (cīvaka. 501)
   82. (pi-m.) 'nāṭārakkaraicēra neṭuṅkoṭimicai naṉkiḻi tarum'
   89. (pi-m.) 'nīrtevunirai'
   tevvukoḷḷutalākiya kuṟippupporuḷai yuṇarttu meṉpataṟku ivvaṭi mēṟkōḷ ;
   tol. uri, cū. 49, cē ; 47, na ; i-vi. cū. 290.
   90. (pi-m.) 'ēttat'
   92. (pi-m.) 'kayamakaiya'
   91-3. āmpi, kiḻāar : "āmpiyuṅ kiḻārum vīṅkicai yēṟṟamum" (cilap.
   10:110)
   89-93. kiḻār pūṭṭaippoṟi yeṉṉum poruḷil varumeṉpataṟku ivvaṭikaḷ mēṟkōḷ
   ; cilap. 10:110, aṭiyār.
   94. (pi-m.) 'atarikoḷpavaricai'
   96. ciṟupāṇ. 148-ām aṭiyaiyum ataṉ kuṟippuraiyaiyum pārkka.
   98. "viḻavuniṉṟa viyaṉmaṟukil" (maturaik. 328)
   --------------
   muḻavuttōṇ muraṭporunark
       kurukeḻu peruñciṟappi         100
       ṉirupeyarp pērāyamo
       ṭilaṅkumaruppiṟ kaḷiṟukoṭuttum
       polantāmaraip pūccūṭṭiyu
       nalañcāṉṟa kalañcitaṟum
       palkuṭṭuvar velkōvē         105
       kalkāyuṅ kaṭuvēṉilo
       ṭiruvāṉam peyaloḷippiṉum
       varumvaikaṉ mīṉpiṟaḻiṉum
       veḷḷamā ṟātu viḷaiyuḷ peruka
       nelli ṉōtai yarinar kampalai         110
       puḷḷimiḻn tolikku micaiyē yeṉṟuñ
       calampukaṉṟu cuṟavukkalitta
       pulavunīr viyaṉpauvattu
       nilavukāṉaṉ muḻavuttāḻaik
       kuḷirppo tumpar naḷittūva         115
       ṉiraitimil vēṭṭuvar karaicēr kampalai
       yiruṅkaḻic ceṟuviṉ veḷḷuppup pakarnaro
       ṭoliyōvāk kaliyāṇar
       mutuveḷḷilai mīkkūṟum
       viyaṉmēval viḻuccelvat         120
       tiruvakaiyā ṉicaicāṉṟa
       ciṟukuṭip peruntoḻuvar
       kuṭikeḻīiya nāṉilavaroṭu
       toṉṟumoḻintu toḻilkēṭpak
       kāleṉṉak kaṭiturāay         125
       nāṭukeṭa veriparappiyālaṅkāṉat tañcuvaraviṟut
       taracupaṭa vamaruḻakki
       muracukoṇṭu kaḷamvēṭṭa
       vaṭutiṟaluyar pukaḻvēntē
       naṭṭavar kuṭiyuyarkkuvai         130
       ceṟṟava raracupeyarkkuvai
       pērulakattu mēentōṉṟic
       cīruṭaiya viḻucciṟappiṉ
       viḷaintumutirnta viḻumutti
       ṉilaṅkuvaḷai yiruñcērik         135
       kaṭkoṇṭik kuṭippākkattu
       naṟkoṟkaiyōr nacaipporuna
       ceṟṟa tevvar kalaṅkat talaicceṉ
       ṟañcuvarat taṭku maṇaṅkuṭait tuppiṟ
       kōḻūuṉkuṟaik koḻuvalcip         140
       pulavuviṟ polikūvai
       yoṉṟumoḻi yoliyiruppiṟ
       ṟeṉparatavar pōrēṟē
       yariyavellā meḷitiṉiṟkoṇ         145
       ṭuriya vellā mōmpātu vīci
       naṉipukaṉ ṟuṟaitu meṉṉā tēṟṟeḻuntu
       paṉivār cimaiyak kāṉam pōki
       yakanāṭu pukkava raruppam vauvi
       yāṇṭupala kaḻiya vēṇṭupulat tiṟuttu         150
       mēmpaṭa marīiya velpōrk kuruci
       luṟuceṟunar pulampukkavar
       kaṭikāvi ṉilaitolaicci
       yiḻipaṟiyāp peruntaṇpaṇai
       kurūukkoṭiya verimēya         155
       nāṭeṉumpēr kāṭāka
       vācēntavaḻi mācēppa
       vūriruntavaḻi pāḻāka
       vilaṅkuvaḷai maṭamaṅkaiyar
       tuṇaṅkaiyañcīrt taḻūumaṟappa         160
       vavaiyirunta perumpotiyiṟ
       kavaiyaṭik kaṭunōkkattup
       pēymakaḷir peyarpāṭa
       vaṇaṅkuvaḻaṅku makalāṅka
       ṇilattāṟṟuṅ kuḻūupputavi         165
       ṉarantaip peṇṭi riṉaintaṉa rakavak
       koḻumpatiya kuṭitēmpic
       ceḻuṅkēḷir niḻalcēra
       neṭunakar vīḻnta karikutirp paḷḷik
       kuṭumik kūkai kurāloṭu muralak         170
       kaḻunīr polinta kaṇṇakaṉ poykaik
       kaḷiṟumāy ceruntiyoṭu kaṇpamaṉ ṟūrtara
       nallēr naṭanta nacaicāl viḷaivayaṟ
       paṉmayirp piṇavoṭu kēḻa lukaḷa
       vāḻā maiyiṉ vaḻitavak keṭṭup         175
       pāḻā yiṉaniṉ pakaivar tēe
       meḻā attō ḷimiḻmuḻakkiṉ
       māattā ḷuyarmaruppiṟ
       kaṭuñciṉatta kaḷiṟuparappi
       virikaṭal viyaṉṟāṉaiyoṭu         180
       murukuṟaḻap pakaittalaicceṉ
       ṟakalvicumpi ṉārppimiḻap
       peyaluṟaḻak kaṇaicitaṟip
       palapuravi nīṟukaippa
       vaḷainarala vayirārppap         185
       pīṭaḻiyak kaṭantaṭṭavar
       nāṭaḻiya veyilvauvic
       cuṟṟamoṭu tūvaṟuttaliṟ
       ceṟṟa tevvar niṉvaḻi naṭappa
       viyaṉkaṇ mutupoḻiṉ maṇṭila muṟṟi         190
       yaraciyal piḻaiyā taṟaneṟi kāṭṭip
       periyōr ceṉṟa vaṭivaḻip piḻaiyātu
       kuṭamutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya toṉṟutoḻu piṟaiyiṉ
       vaḻivaḻic ciṟakkaniṉ valampaṭu koṟṟaṅ
       kuṇamutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya vāriruṇ matiyiṟ         195
       ṟēyvaṉa keṭukaniṉ ṟevva rākka
       muyarnilai yulaka mamiḻtoṭu peṟiṉum
       poycē ṇīṅkiya vāynaṭ piṉaiyē
       muḻaṅkukaṭa lēṇi malartalai yulakamo
       ṭuyarnta tēettu viḻumiyōr variṉum         200

   pakaivark kañcip paṇintoḻu kalaiyē
       teṉpula maruṅkiṉ viṇṭu niṟaiya
       vāṇaṉ vaitta viḻuniti peṟiṉum
       paḻinamak keḻuka veṉṉāy viḻuniti
       yīta luḷḷamo ṭicaivēṭ kuvaiyē         205
       yaṉṉāy niṉṉoṭu muṉṉilai yevaṉō
       koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuva laṭupō raṇṇal
       kēṭṭiciṉ vāḻi keṭukaniṉ ṉavalaṅ
       keṭātu nilaiiyarniṉ cēṇviḷakku nallicai
       tavapperukkat taṟāyāṇa         210
       raḻittāṉāk koḻuntiṟṟi
       yiḻittāṉāp palacoṉṟi
       yuṇṭāṉāk kūrnaṟaviṟ
       ṟiṉṟāṉā viṉavaika
       ṉilaṉeṭuk kallā voṇpal veṟukkaip         215
       payaṉaṟa vaṟiyā vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar
       narampiṉ muralu nayamvaru muraṟci
       viṟaliyar vaṟuṅkaik kuṟuntoṭi ceṟippap
       pāṇa ruvappak kaḷiṟupala tarī ik
       kalantō ruvappa veyiṟpala kaṭaii         220
       maṟaṅkalaṅkat talaicceṉṟu
       vāḷuḻantataṉ ṟāḷvāḻtti
       nāḷīṇṭiya nallakavarkkut
       tērōṭu mācitaṟic
       cūṭuṟṟa cuṭarppūviṉ         225
       pāṭupularnta naṟuñcāntiṉ
       viḻumiya periyōr cuṟṟa mākak
       kaḷḷi ṉirumpaik kalañcela vuṇṭu
       paṇintōr tēen tamvaḻi naṭappap
       paṇiyār tēem paṇittuttiṟai koṇmār         230
       paruntupaṟak kallāp pārvaṟ pācaṟaip
       paṭukaṇ muracaṅ kālai yiyampa
       veṭipaṭak kaṭantu vēṇṭupulat tiṟutta
       paṇaikeḻu peruntiṟaṟ palvēṉ maṉṉar
       karaiporu tiraṅkuṅ kaṉaiyiru munnīrt         235
       tiraiyiṭu maṇaliṉum palarē yuraicela
       malar talai yulaka māṇṭukaḻin tōrē
       ataṉāl, kuṇakaṭal koṇṭu kuṭakaṭaṉ muṟṟi
       yiravu mellaiyum viḷiviṭa ṉaṟiyā
       tavalu micaiyu nīrttiraḷ pīṇṭik         240
       kavalaiyaṅ kuḻumpi ṉaruvi yolippak
       kaḻaivaḷar cāraṟ kaḷiṟṟiṉa naṭuṅka
       varaimuta liraṅku mēṟoṭu vāṉñemirntu
       citaraṟ perumpeyal tiṟattaliṟ ṟāṅkātu
       kuṇakaṭaṟ kivar taruṅ kurūuppuṉa lunti         245
       nivantuce ṉīttaṅ kuḷaṅkoḷac cāṟṟik
       kaḷiṟumāykkuṅ katirkkaḻaṉi
       yoḷiṟilañci yaṭainivanta
       muṭṭāḷa cuṭarttāmarai
       kaṭkamaḻu naṟuneytal         250
       vaḷḷita ḻaviḻnīla
       mellilai yariyāmpaloṭu
       vaṇṭiṟai koṇṭa kamaḻpūm poykaik
       kampuṭ cēva liṉṟuyi liriya
       vaḷḷai nīkki vayamīṉ mukantu         255
       koḷḷai cāṟṟiya koṭumuṭi valaiñar
       vēḻap paḻaṉattu nūḻi lāṭṭuk
       karumpi ṉentiraṅ kaṭpi ṉōtai
       yaḷḷaṟ ṟaṅkiya pakaṭuṟu viḻumaṅ
       kaḷḷār kaḷamar peyarkku mārppē         260
       yolinta pakaṉṟai viḷainta kaḻaṉi
       vaṉkai viṉaiña raripaṟai yiṉkuraṟ
       ṟaḷimaḻai poḻiyun taṇparaṅ kuṉṟiṟ
       kalikoḷ cummai yoliko ḷāyan
       tatainta kōtai tāroṭu poliyap         265
       puṇantuṭa ṉāṭu micaiyē yaṉaittu
       makaliru vāṉat timiḻntiṉi ticaippak
       kurukunarala maṉaimarattāṉ
       mīṉcīvum pāṇcēriyoṭu
       marutañ cāṉṟa taṇpaṇai cuṟṟi yorucārc         270
       ciṟutiṉai koyyak kavvai kaṟuppak
       karuṅkāl varaki ṉiruṅkural pulara
       vāḻnta kuḻumpiṟ ṟirumaṇi kiḷara
       veḻunta kaṭaṟṟi ṉaṉpoṉ koḻippap
       peruṅkaviṉ peṟṟa ciṟutalai nauvi         275
       maṭakkaṭ piṇaiyoṭu maṟukuvaṉa vukaḷac
       cuṭarppūṅ koṉṟai tāaya nīḻaṟ
       pāa yaṉṉa pāṟai yaṇintu
       nīlat taṉṉa paimpayir micaitoṟum
       veḷḷi yaṉṉa voḷvī yutirntu         280
       curimukiḻ mucuṇṭaiyoṭu mullai tāay
       maṇimaru ṇeyta luṟaḻak kāmar
       tuṇinīr mellavaṟ ṟoyyiloṭu malara
       vallōṉ ṟaiiya veṟikkaḷaṅ kaṭuppa
       mullai cāṉṟa puṟavaṇin torucār         285
       naṟuṅkāḻ koṉṟu kōṭṭiṉ vittiya
       kuṟuṅkatirt tōrai neṭuṅkā laiyavi
       yaivaṉa veṇṇelo ṭarilkoḷpu nīṭi
       yiñci mañcaṭ paiṅkaṟi piṟavum
       palvēṟu tāramoṭu kallakat tīṇṭit         290
       tiṉaiviḷai cāraṟ kiḷikaṭi pūcaṉ
       maṇippū vavaraik kurūuttaḷir mēyu
       māmā kaṭiyuṅ kāṉavar pūcal
       cēṇō ṉakaḻnta maṭivāyp payampiṉ
       vīḻmukak kēḻa laṭṭa pūcal         295
       karuṅkāl vēṅkai yiruñciṉaip poṅkar
       naṟumpūk koyyum pūca liruṅkē
       ḻēṟaṭu vayappulip pūcalo ṭaṉaittu
       milaṅkuveḷ ḷaruviyoṭu cilampakat tiraṭṭak
       karuṅkāṟ kuṟiñci cāṉṟa veṟpaṇin         300

   taruṅkaṭi māmalai taḻīi yorucā
       riruvetirp paintūṟu kūreri naippa
       niḻatta yāṉai mēypulam paṭarak
       kalitta viyava riyantoṭ ṭaṉṉa
       kaṇviṭu puṭaiyūut taṭṭai kaviṉaḻin         305
       taruvi yāṉṟa vaṇiyiṉ māmalai
       vaikaṇ ṭaṉṉa puṉmuḷi yaṅkāṭṭuk
       kamañcūḻ kōṭai viṭaraka mukantu
       kāluṟu kaṭali ṉolikkuñ cummai
       yilavēy kurampai yuḻaiyataṭ paḷḷi         310
       yuvalaik kaṇṇi vaṉco liḷaiñar
       cilaiyuṭaik kaiyar kavalai kāppa
       niḻaluru viḻanta vēṉiṟkuṉ ṟattup
       pālai cāṉṟa curañcērn torucār
       muḻaṅkukaṭa ṟanta viḷaṅkukatir mutta         315
       marampōḻn taṟutta kaṇṇē rilaṅkuvaḷai
       paratar tanta palvēṟu kūla
       miruṅkaḻic ceṟuviṟ ṟīmpuḷi veḷḷuppup
       parantōṅku varaippiṉ vaṉkait timilar
       koḻumīṉ kuṟaiiya tuṭikkaṭ ṭuṇiyal         320
       viḻumiya nāvāy perunī rōccunar
       naṉantalait tēettu naṉkala ṉuymmār
       puṇarntuṭaṉ koṇarnta puraviyo ṭaṉaittum
       vaika ṟōṟum vaḻivaḻic ciṟappa
       neytal cāṉṟa vaḷampala payiṉṟāṅ         325
       kaimpāṟ ṟiṇaiyuṅ kaviṉi yamaivara
       muḻavimiḻu makalāṅkaṇ
       viḻavuniṉṟa viyaṉmaṟukiṟ
       ṟuṇaṅkaiyan taḻūuviṉ maṇaṅkamaḻ cēri
       yiṉkali yāṇark kuḻūuppala payiṉṟāṅkup         330
       pāṭal cāṉṟa naṉṉāṭṭu naṭuvaṭ
       kalaitāya vuyarcimaiyattu
       mayilakavu malipoṅkar
       manti yāṭa māvicum pukantu
       muḻaṅkukāl poruta marampayil kāvi         335
       ṉiyaṅkupuṉal koḻitta veṇṭalaik kuvavumaṇaṟ
       kāṉpoḻi ṟaḻī iya vaṭaikarai tōṟun
       tātucūḻ kōṅkiṉ pūmalar tāayk
       kōtaiyi ṉoḻakum virinīr nalvara
       laviraṟal vaiyait tuṟaituṟai tōṟum         340
       palvēṟu pūttiraṭ ṭaṇṭalai cuṟṟi
       yaḻuntupaṭ ṭirunta perumpā ṇirukkaiyu
       nilaṉum vaḷaṉuṅ kaṇṭamai kallā
       viḷaṅkuperun tiruviṉ māṉa viṟalvē
       ḷaḻumpi laṉṉa nāṭiḻan taṉaruṅ         345
       koḻumpal patiya kuṭiyiḻan taṉarun
       toṉṟukaṟut tuṟaiyun tupputtara vanta
       vaṇṇal yāṉai yaṭupōr vēnta
       riṉṉicai muraca miṭaippulat toḻiyap
       paṉmā ṟōṭṭip peyarpuṟam peṟṟu         350
       maṇṇuṟa vāḻnta maṇinīrk kiṭaṅkiṉ
       viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiya palpaṭaip puricait
       tolvali nilaiiya vaṇaṅkuṭai neṭunilai
       neypaṭak karinta tiṇpōrk kataviṉ
       maḻaiyāṭu malaiyi ṉivanta māṭamoṭu         355
       vaiyai yaṉṉa vaḻakkuṭai vāyil
       vakaipeṟa veḻuntu vāṉa mūḻkic
       cilkāṟ ṟicaikkum palpuḻai nallil
       yāṟukiṭan taṉṉa vakaṉeṭun teruviṟ
       palvēṟu kuḻāat ticaiyeḻun tolippa         360
       mākā leṭutta munnīr pōla
       muḻaṅkicai naṉpaṇai yaṟaivaṉar nuvalak
       kayaṅkuṭain taṉṉa viyantoṭ ṭimiḻicai
       makiḻntō rāṭuṅ kalikoḷ cummai
       yōvukkaṇ ṭaṉṉa viruperu niyamattuc         365
       cāṟayarn teṭutta vuruvap palkoṭi
       vēṟupal peyara vāreyil koḷakkoḷa
       nāṭō ṟeṭutta nalampeṟu puṉaikoṭi
       nīrolit taṉṉa nilavuvēṟ ṟāṉaiyoṭu
       pulavuppaṭak koṉṟu miṭaitō lōṭṭip         370
       pukaḻcey teṭutta viṟalcā ṉaṉkoṭi
       kaḷḷiṉ kaḷinavil koṭiyoṭu naṉpala
       palvēṟu kuḻūukkoṭi patākai nilaiipa
       peruvarai maruṅki ṉaruviyi ṉuṭaṅkap
       paṉaimīṉ vaḻaṅkum vaḷaimēy parappiṉ         375
       vīṅkupiṇi nōṉkayi ṟarīi yitaipuṭaiyūk
       kūmpumutaṉ muruṅka veṟṟik kāyntuṭaṉ
       kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṭuppak kalporu turaii
       neṭuñcuḻip paṭṭa nāvāy pōla
       virutalaip paṇila mārppac ciṉañciṟantu         380
       kōlōrk koṉṟu mēlōr vīci
       meṉpiṇi vaṉṟoṭar pēṇātu kāḻcāyttuk
       kantunīt tuḻitaruṅ kaṭāa yāṉaiyu
       maṅkaṇmāl vicumpu pukaiyavaḷipōḻn
       toṇkatir ñāyiṟ ṟū ṟaḷavāt tiritaruñ         385
       ceṅkā laṉṉattuc cēva laṉṉa
       kurūumayirp puravi yurāliṟ parinimirntu
       kāleṉak kaṭukkuṅ kaviṉpeṟu tēruṅ
       koṇṭa kōlaṉ koḷkai naviṟṟali
       ṉaṭipaṭu maṇṭilat tāti pōkiya         390
       koṭipaṭu cuvala viṭumayirp puraviyum
       vēḻat taṉṉa veruvaru celaviṟ
       kaḷḷār kaḷama riruñceru mayakkamu
       mariyavum periyavum varuvaṉa peyartaliṟ
       ṟīmpuḻal valcik kaḻaṟkāṉ maḻavar         395
       pūntalai muḻavi ṉōṉṟalai kaṭuppap
       piṭakaip peyta kamaḻnaṟum pūviṉar
       palavakai viritta vetirpūṅ kōtaiyar
       palartoku piṭitta tātuku cuṇṇattar

   takaicey tīñcēṟ ṟiṉṉīrp pacuṅkāy         400
       nīṭukoṭi yilaiyiṉar kōṭucuṭu nūṟṟiṉa
       rirutalai vanta pakaimuṉai kaṭuppa
       viṉṉuyi rañci yiṉṉā veytuyirt
       tēṅkuvaṉa riruntavai nīṅkiya piṉṟaip
       palvēṟu paṇṇiyan taḻīittari vilaiñar         405
       malaipurai māṭattuk koḻuniḻa liruntara
       viruṅkaṭal vāṉkōṭu puraiya vāruṟṟup
       perumpiṉ ṉiṭṭa vāṉaraik kūntalar
       naṉṉar nalattar toṉmutu peṇṭir
       cennīrp pacumpoṉ puṉainta pāvai         410
       celcuṭarp pacuveyiṟ ṟōṉṟi yaṉṉa
       ceyyar ceyirtta nōkkiṉar maṭakka
       ṇaiiya kaluḻu māmaiyar vaiyeyiṟṟu
       vārnta vāyar vaṇaṅkiṟaip paṇaittōṭ
       cōrntuku vaṉṉa vayakkuṟu vantikait         415
       toyyil poṟitta cuṇaṅketi riḷamulai
       maiyuk kaṉṉa moyyiruṅ kūntaṉ
       mayiliya lōru maṭamoḻi yōruṅ
       kaii melliti ṉotuṅkik kaiyeṟintu
       kallā māntaroṭu nakuvaṉar tiḷaippap         420
       puṭaiyamai polinta vakaiyamai ceppiṟ
       kāma ruruviṟ ṟāmvēṇṭu paṇṇiyaṅ
       kamaḻnaṟum pūvoṭu maṉaimaṉai maṟuka
       maḻaikoḷak kuṟaiyātu puṉalpuka mikātu
       karaiporu tiraṅku munnīr pōlak         425
       koḷakkoḷak kuṟaiyātu tarattara mikātu
       kaḻunīr koṇṭa veḻunā ḷanti
       yāṭutuvaṉṟu viḻavi ṉāṭārt taṉṟē
       māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭa
       ṉāḷaṅ kāṭi naṉantalaik kampalai         430
       veyiṟkatir maḻuṅkiya paṭarkūr ñāyiṟṟuc
       cekka raṉṉa civantunuṇaṅ kuruviṟ
       kaṇporu pukūu moṇpūṅ kaliṅkam
       poṉpuṉai vāḷoṭu poliyak kaṭṭit
       tiṇṭōp pirampiṟ puraḷun tāṉaik         435
       kaccan tiṉṟa kaḻaṟayaṅku tiruntaṭi
       moympiṟantu tiritaru moruperunteriyaṉ
       maṇitoṭarn taṉṉa voṇpūṅ kōtai
       yaṇikiḷar mārpi ṉāramo ṭaḷaiik
       kāliyak kaṉṉa kataḻpari kaṭaiik         440
       kālōr kāppak kāleṉak kaḻiyum
       vāṉa vaṇkai valaṅkeḻu celvar
       nāṇmaki ḻirukkai kāṇmār pūṇoṭu
       teḷḷarip poṟcilam polippa voḷḷaḻaṟ
       ṟāvaṟa viḷaṅkiya vāypo ṉaviriḻai         445
       yaṇaṅkuvīḻ vaṉṉa pūntoṭi makaḷir
       maṇaṅkamaḻ nāṟṟan teruvuṭaṉ kamaḻa
       voṇkuḻai tikaḻu moḷikeḻu tirumukan
       tiṇkā ḻēṟṟa viyaliru vilōtan
       teṇkaṭaṟ ṟiraiyi ṉacaivaḷi puṭaippa         450
       nirainilai māṭat taramiyan tōṟu
       maḻaimāy matiyiṟ ṟōṉṟupu maṟaiya
       nīru nilaṉun tīyum vaḷiyu
       māka vicumpō ṭaintuṭa ṉiyaṟṟiya
       maḻuvā ṇeṭiyōṉ ṟalaiva ṉāka         455
       mācaṟa viḷaṅkiya yākkaiyar cūḻcuṭar
       vāṭāp pūvi ṉimaiyā nāṭṭattu
       nāṟṟa vuṇavi ṉurukeḻu periyōrkku
       māṟṟaru marapi ṉuyarpali koṭumā
       ranti viḻaviṟ ṟūriyaṅ kaṟaṅkat         460
       tiṇkatir matāṇi yoṇkuṟu mākkaḷai
       yōmpiṉart taḻīit tāmpuṇarntu muyaṅkit
       tātaṇi tāmaraip pōtupiṭit tāṅkut
       tāmu mavaru mōrāṅku viḷaṅkak
       kāmar kaviṉiya pēriḷam peṇṭir         465
       pūviṉar pukaiyiṉar toḻuvaṉar paḻiccic
       ciṟantupuṟaṅ kākkuṅ kaṭavuṭ paḷḷiyuñ
       ciṟanta vētam viḷaṅkappāṭi
       viḻuccī reytiya voḻukkamoṭu puṇarntu
       nilamamar vaiyat torutā māki         470
       yuyarnilai yulaka mivaṇiṉ ṟeytu
       maṟaneṟi piḻaiyā vaṉpuṭai neñciṟ
       periyōr mēe yiṉiti ṉuṟaiyuṅ
       kuṉṟukuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa vantaṇar paḷḷiyum
       vaṇṭupaṭap paḻuniya tēṉār tōṟṟattup         475
       pūvum pukaiyuñ cāvakar paḻiccac
       ceṉṟa kālamum varūu mamayamu
       miṉṟivaṭ ṭōṉṟiya voḻukkamoṭu naṉ kuṇarntu
       vāṉamu nilaṉun tāmuḻu tuṇaruñ
       cāṉṟa koḷkaic cāyā yākkai         480
       yāṉṟaṭaṅ kaṟiñar ceṟintaṉar nōṉmār
       kalpoḷin taṉṉa viṭṭuvāyk karaṇṭaip
       palpuric cimili nāṟṟi nalkuvarak
       kayaṅkaṇ ṭaṉṉa vayaṅkuṭai nakarattuc
       cempiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa ceñcuvar puṉaintu         485
       nōkkuvicai tavirppa mēkkuyarn tōṅki
       yiṟumpūtu cāṉṟa naṟumpūñ cēkkaiyuṅ
       kuṉṟupala kuḻīip polivāṉa tōṉṟa
       vaccamu mavalamu mārvamu nīkkic
       ceṟṟamu muvakaiyuñ ceyyātu kāttu         490
       ñemaṉkō laṉṉa cemmait tākic
       ciṟanta koḷkai yaṟaṅkū ṟavaiyamu
       naṟuñcāntu nīviya kēḻkiḷa rakalat
       tāvuti maṇṇi yavir tukiṉ muṭittu
       māvicumpu vaḻaṅkum periyōr pōla         495
       naṉṟun tītuṅ kaṇṭāyn taṭakki
       yaṉpu maṟaṉu moḻiyātu kāttup
       paḻiyorīi yuyarntu pāypukaḻ niṟainta
       cemmai cāṉṟa kāviti mākkaḷu
       maṟaneṟi piḻaiyā tāṟṟi ṉoḻukik         500

   kuṟumpal kuḻuviṟ kuṉṟukaṇ ṭaṉṉa
       paruntirun tukakkum paṉmā ṇalliṟ
       palvēṟu paṇṭamō ṭūṇmalintu kaviṉi
       malaiyavu nilattavu nīravum piṟavum
       palvēṟu tirumaṇi muttamoṭu poṉkoṇṭu         505
       ciṟanta tēettup paṇṇiyam pakarnaru
       maḻaiyoḻuk kaṟāap piḻaiyā viḷaiyuṭ
       paḻaiyaṉ mōkū ravaiyakam viḷaṅka;
       nāṉmoḻik kōcar tōṉṟi yaṉṉa
       tāmēen tōṉṟiya nāṟperuṅ kuḻuvuṅ         510
       kōṭupōḻ kaṭainarun tirumaṇi kuyiṉaruñ
       cūṭuṟu naṉpoṉ cuṭariḻai puṉainarum
       poṉṉurai kāṇmaruṅ kaliṅkam pakarnaruñ
       cempuniṟai koṇmarum vampuniṟai muṭinarum
       pūvum pukaiyu māyu mākkaḷu         515
       mevvakaic ceytiyu muvamaṅ kāṭṭi
       nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarnta nuḻainta nōkkiṟ
       kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñarum piṟaruṅ kūṭit
       teṇṭirai yaviraṟal kaṭuppa voṇpal
       kuṟiyavu neṭiyavu maṭitarūu virittuc         520
       ciṟiyarum periyaruṅ kammiyar kuḻīi
       nālvēṟu teruviṉuṅ kāluṟa niṟṟarak
       koṭumpaṟaik kōṭiyar kaṭumpuṭaṉ vāḻttun
       taṇkaṭa ṉāṭa ṉoṇpūṅ kōtai
       perunā ḷirukkai viḻumiyōr kuḻīi         525
       viḻaivukoḷ kampalai kaṭuppap palavuṭaṉ
       cēṟu nāṟṟamum palaviṉ cuḷaiyum;
       vēṟupaṭak kaviṉiya tēmāṅ kaṉiyum
       pavvē ṟuruviṟ kāyum paḻaṉuṅ
       koṇṭal vaḷarppak koṭiviṭupu kaviṉi         530
       meṉpiṇi yaviḻnta kuṟumuṟi yaṭaku
       mamirtiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa tīñcēṟṟuk kaṭikaiyum
       pukaḻpaṭap paṇṇiya pērūṉ cōṟuṅ
       kīḻcela vīḻnta kiḻaṅkoṭu piṟavu
       miṉcōṟu tarunar palvayi ṉukara         535
       vālitai yeṭutta vaḷitaru vaṅkam
       palvēṟu paṇṭa miḻitarum paṭṭiṉat
       tolle ṉimiḻicai māṉak kalleṉa
       naṉantalai viṉaiñar kalaṅkoṇṭu maṟukap
       peruṅkaṭaṟ kuṭṭattup pulavuttirai yōta         540
       miruṅkaḻi maruvip pāyap periteḻun
       turukeḻu pāṉāḷ varuvaṉa peyartaliṟ
       palvēṟu puḷḷi ṉicaiyeḻun taṟṟē
       yallaṅ kāṭi yaḻitaru kampalai
       yoṇcuṭa ruruppoḷi maḻuṅkac ciṉantaṇintu         545
       ceṉṟa ñāyiṟu naṉpakaṟ koṇṭu
       kuṭamutaṟ kuṉṟañ cērak kuṇamuta
       ṉāṇmutir matiyan tōṉṟi nilāviripu
       pakaluru vuṟṟa viravuvara nayantōr
       kāta liṉṟuṇai puṇarmā rāyitaḻt         550
       taṇṇaṟuṅ kaḻunīr tuṇaippa viḻaipuṉaiyūu
       naṉṉeṭuṅ kūnta ṉaṟuvirai kuṭaiya
       naranta maraippa naṟuñcāntu maṟuka
       meṉṉūṟ kaliṅkaṅ kamaḻpukai maṭuppak
       peṇmakiḻ vuṟṟa piṇainōkku makaḷir         555
       neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkaṅ koḷīi neṭunaka
       rellai yallā nōyoṭu pukuntu
       kalleṉ mālai nīṅka nāṇukkoḷa
       vēḻpuṇar ciṟappi ṉiṉṟoṭaic cīṟiyāḻ
       tāḻpayaṟ kaṉaikural kaṭuppap paṇṇuppeyarttu         560
       vīḻtuṇai taḻīi viyalvicumpu kamaḻa
       nīrtiraṇ ṭaṉṉa kōtai piṟakkiṭ
       ṭāykō lavirtoṭi viḷaṅka vīcip
       pōtaviḻ putumalar teruvuṭaṉ kamaḻa
       mētaku takaiya mikunala meytip         565
       perumpal kuvaḷaic curumpupaṭu paṉmalar;
       tiṟantumōn taṉṉa ciṟantukamaḻ nāṟṟattuk
       koṇṭaṉ malarpputaṉ māṉappū vēyntu
       nuṇpū ṇākam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅki
       māyap poypala kūṭṭik kavavukkarantu         570
       cēyaru naṇiyaru nalaṉayantu vanta
       viḷampal celvar vaḷantapa vāṅki
       nuṇṭā tuṇṭu vaṟumpūt tuṟakku
       meṉciṟai vaṇṭiṉa māṉap puṇarntōr
       neñcē māppa viṉṟuyi ṟuṟantu         575
       paḻantēr vāḻkkaip paṟavai pōlak
       koḻuṅkuṭic celvarum piṟaru mēeya
       maṇampuṇarn tōṅkiya vaṇaṅkuṭai nalli
       lāypoṉ ṉavirtoṭip pāciḻai makaḷi
       roṇcuṭar viḷakkattup palaruṭaṉ ṟuvaṉṟi         580
       nīṉiṟa vicumpi lamarntaṉa rāṭum
       vāṉava makaḷir māṉak kaṇṭōr
       neñcu naṭukkuṟūuk koṇṭi makaḷir
       yāma nalyāḻ nāppa ṇiṉṟa
       muḻaviṉ makiḻntaṉa rāṭik kuṇṭunīrp         585
       paṉittuṟaik kuvavumaṇaṉ muṉaii meṉṟaḷirk
       koḻuṅkompu koḻuti nīrnaṉai mēvara
       neṭuntoṭark kuvaḷai vaṭimpuṟa vaṭaicci
       maṇaṅkamaḻ maṉaitoṟum poyta layarak
       kaṇaṅko ḷavuṇark kaṭanta polantār         590
       māyōṉ mēya vōṇa naṉṉāṭ
       kōṇan tiṉṟa vaṭuvāḻ mukatta
       cāṇan tiṉṟa camantāṅku taṭakkai
       maṟaṅkoḷ cēri māṟuporu ceruviṉ
       māṟā tuṟṟa vaṭuppaṭu neṟṟic         595
       curumpār kaṇṇip perumpukaṉ maṟavar
       kaṭuṅkaḷi ṟōṭṭaliṟ kāṇuna riṭṭa
       neṭuṅkaraik kāḻaka nilampara luṟuppak
       kaṭuṅkaṭ ṭēṟaṉ makiḻciṟantu tiritarak
       kaṇava ruvappap putalvarp payantu         600

   (malaipaṭu. 128) ; "kiḻaṅkukīḻ vīḻntu" (naṟ. 328:1) ; "vaḷḷikīḻ vīḻā"
   (kalit. 39:12) ; "koḻuṅkaṭi vaḷḷik kiḻaṅku vīḻk kummē" (puṟanā. 109:6)
   540. kaṭaṟkuṭṭam ; "kaṭaṟkuṭṭam pōḻvar kalavar" (nāṉmaṇik. 16)
   543-4. "paḻumarat tīṇṭiya paṟavaiyi ṉeḻūu, miḻumeṉ cummai" (maṇi.
   14:26-7) ; "muṭṭilā mūvaṟu pāṭai mākkaḷāṟ, puṭpayil paḻumarap poliviṟ
   ṟākiya, maṭṭilā vaḷanakar", "cīrkeḻuvaḷamaṉai tiḷaittu mācaṉam
   ...................... pārkeḻu paḻumarap paṟavai yottavē" (cīvaka. 93,
   828) : "kaḷaṉavi laṉṉamē mutala kaṇṇakaṉ, ṟaḷamalarp puḷḷoṭu tayaṅki
   yiṉṉatōr, kiḷaviyeṉ ṟaṟivaruṅ kiḷarccit tātalāṉ,
   vaḷanakark kūlamē pōlu māṇpatu" (kampa. pampai. 7)
   548-9. nilāviripu pakaluruvuṟṟa iravu : "pakaluru vuṟaḻnilāk kāṉṟu

   paṇaittēn tiḷamulai yamuta mūṟap
       pulavuppuṉiṟu tīrntu polinta cuṟṟamoṭu
       vaḷamaṉai makaḷir kuḷanī rayarat
       tivavumeyn niṟuttuc cevvaḻi paṇṇik
       kuralpuṇar nalyāḻ muḻavō ṭoṉṟi         605
       nuṇṇī rākuḷi yiraṭṭap palavuṭa
       ṉoṇcuṭar viḷakka muntuṟa maṭaiyoṭu
       naṉmā mayiliṉ meṉmela viyalik
       kaṭuñcūṉ makaḷir pēṇik kaitoḻutu
       peruntōṭ cāliṉi maṭuppa vorucā         610
       raruṅkaṭi vēlaṉ murukoṭu vaḷaii
       yarikkū ṭiṉṉiyaṅ kaṟaṅkanēr niṟuttuk
       kārmalark kuṟiñci cūṭik kaṭampiṉ
       cīrmiku neṭuvēṭ pēṇit taḻūuppiṇaiyūu
       maṉṟutoṟu niṉṟa kuravai cēritoṟu         615
       muraiyum pāṭṭu māṭṭum viraii
       vēṟuvēṟu kampalai veṟikoḷpu mayaṅkip
       pēricai naṉṉaṉ peṟumpeyar naṉṉāṭ
       cēri viḻavi ṉārppeḻun tāṅku
       muntai yāmañ ceṉṟa piṉṟaip         620
       paṇilaṅ kaliyavin taṭaṅkak kāḻcāyttu
       noṭainavi ṉeṭuṅkaṭai yaṭaittu maṭamata
       roḷḷiḻai makaḷir paḷḷi yayara
       nalvari yiṟāal puraiyu mellaṭai
       yayirurup puṟṟa vāṭamai vicayaṅ         625
       kavavoṭu piṭitta vakaiyamai mōtakan
       tīñcēṟṟuk kūviyar tūṅkuvaṉa ruṟaṅka
       viḻavi ṉāṭum vayiriyar maṭiyap
       pāṭāṉ ṟavinta paṉikkaṭal puraiyap
       pāyal vaḷarvōr kaṇṇiṉitu maṭuppap         630
       pāṉāṭ koṇṭa kaṅku liṭaiyatu
       pēyu maṇaṅku miruvukoṇ ṭāykōṟ
       kūṟṟak koḵṟēr kaḻutoṭu koṭpa
       virumpiṭi mēentō laṉṉa viruḷcērpu
       kallu maraṉun tuṇikkuṅ kūrmait         635
       toṭalai vāḷar toṭutō laṭiyar
       kuṟaṅkiṭaip patitta kūrnuṉaik kuṟumpiṭic
       ciṟanta karumai nuṇviṉai nuṇaṅkaṟa
       ṉiṟaṅkavarpu puṉainta nīlak kacciṉar
       meṉṉū lēṇip paṉmāṇ cuṟṟiṉar         640
       nilaṉaka ḻuḷiyar kalaṉacaiik koṭkuṅ
       kaṇmā ṟāṭava roṭukka moṟṟi
       vayakkaḷiṟu pārkkum vayappuli pōlat
       tuñcāk kaṇṇa rañcāk koḷkaiya
       raṟintōr pukaḻnta vāṇmaiyar ceṟinta         645
       nūlvaḻip piḻaiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ ṭērcci
       yūrkāp pāḷa rūkkaruṅ kaṇaiyiṉar
       tērvaḻakku teruvi ṉīrtiraṇ ṭoḻuka
       maḻaiyamain tuṟṟa varainā ḷamayamu
       macaivila reḻuntu nayamvantu vaḻaṅkaliṟ         650
       kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkuṅ kaiyaṟu kaṅkulu
       macca maṟiyā tēma mākiya
       maṟṟai yāmam pakaluṟak kaḻippip
       pōtupiṇi viṭṭa kamaḻnaṟum poykait
       tātuṇ ṭumpi pōtu muraṉṟāṅ         655
       kōta lantaṇar vētam pāṭac
       cīriṉitu koṇṭu narampiṉi tiyakki
       yāḻōr marutam paṇṇak kāḻōr
       kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu kavaḷaṅ kaippa neṭuntērpa
       paṇainilaip puravi pulluṇāt teviṭṭap         660
       palvēṟu paṇṇiyak kaṭaimeḻuk kuṟuppak
       kaḷḷōr kaḷinoṭai nuvala villōr
       nayanta kātalar kavavuppiṇit tuñcip
       pularntuviri viṭiya leyta virumpik
       kaṇporā veṟikku miṉṉukkoṭi puraiya         665
       voṇpo ṉaviriḻai teḻippa viyalit
       tiṇcuvar nalliṟ katavaṅ karaiya
       vuṇṭumakiḻ taṭṭa maḻalai nāviṟ
       paḻañceruk kāḷar taḻaṅkukura ṟōṉṟac
       cūtar vāḻtta mākatar nuvala         670
       vētā ḷikaroṭu nāḻikai yicaippa
       vimiḻmura ciraṅka vēṟumāṟu cilaippap
       poṟimayir vāraṇam vaikaṟai yiyampa
       yāṉaiyaṅ kurukiṉ cēvaloṭu kāma
       raṉṉaṅ karaiya vaṇimayi lakavap         675
       piṭipuṇar peruṅkaḷiṟu muḻaṅka muḻuvalik
       kūṭṭuṟai vayamāp puliyoṭu kuḻuma
       vāṉa nīṅkiya nīṉiṟa vicumpiṉ
       miṉṉunimirn taṉaiya rāki naṟavumakiḻntu
       māṇiḻai makaḷir pulantaṉar parinta         680
       parūukkā ḻārañ corinta muttamoṭu
       poṉcuṭu neruppi ṉilamuk keṉṉa
       vammeṉ kurummaip kāypaṭupu piṟavun
       tarumaṇaṉ muṟṟat tariñimi ṟārppa
       meṉpūñ cemmaloṭu naṉkalañ cīppa         685
       viravuttalaip peyaru mēma vaikaṟai
       maipaṭu peruntōṇ maḻava rōṭṭi
       yiṭaippulat toḻinta vēntukōṭ ṭiyāṉai
       pakaippulaṅ kavarnta pāyparip puravi
       vēlkō lāka vāḷcela nūṟik         690
       kāyciṉa muṉpiṟ kaṭuṅkaṭ kūḷiya
       rūrcuṭu viḷakkiṟ ṟanta vāyamu
       nāṭuṭai nalleyi laṇaṅkuṭait tōṭṭi
       nāṭoṟum viḷaṅkak kaitoḻūup paḻicci
       nāṭara vanta viḻukkala maṉaittuṅ         695
       kaṅkaiyam pēriyāṟu kaṭaṟpaṭarn tāaṅ
       kaḷantukaṭai yaṟiyā vaḷaṅkeḻu tāramoṭu
       puttē ḷulakaṅ kaviṉik kāṇvara
       mikkuppuka ḻeytiya perumpeyar maturaik
       ciṉaitalai maṇanta curumpupaṭu cennī         700

   yoṇpūm piṇṭi yaviḻnta kāviṟ
       cuṭarpoḻin tēṟiya viḷaṅkukatir ñāyiṟ
       ṟilaṅkukati riḷaveyiṟ ṟōṉṟi yaṉṉa
       tamaṉiyam vaḷaiiya tāvil viḷaṅkiḻai
       nilamviḷak kuṟuppa mētakap polintu         705
       mayilō raṉṉa cāyaṉ māviṉ
       taḷirē raṉṉa mēṉit taḷirppuṟat
       tīrkki ṉarumpiya titalaiyar kūreyiṟ
       ṟoṇkuḻai puṇariya vaṇṭāḻ kātiṟ
       kaṭavuṭkayat tamaṉṟa cuṭaritaḻt tāmarait         710
       tātupaṭu perumpōtu puraiyum vāṇmukat
       tāytoṭi makaḷir naṟuntōḷ puṇarntu
       kōtaiyiṟ polinta cēkkait tuñcit
       tiruntutuyi leṭuppa viṉiti ṉeḻuntu
       tiṇkā ḻāra nīvik katirviṭu         715
       moṇkā ḻāraṅ kavaiiya mārpiṉ
       varikkaṭaip piraca mūcuvaṉa moyppa
       veruttan tāḻnta viravuppūn teriyaṟ
       polañceyap polinta nalampeṟu viḷakkam
       valikeḻu taṭakkait toṭiyoṭu cuṭarvarac         720
       cōṟamai vuṟṟa nīruṭaik kaliṅka
       muṭaiyaṇi poliyak kuṟaiviṉṟu kavaii
       vallōṉ ṟaiiya varippuṉai pāvai
       murukiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa vuruviṉai yāki
       varupuṇaṟ kaṟciṟai kaṭuppa viṭaiyaṟut         725
       toṉṉā rōṭṭiya ceruppukaṉ maṟavar
       vāḷvalam puṇarntaniṉ ṟāḷvalam vāḻtta
       villaik kavaiik kaṇaitāṅku mārpiṉ
       mātāṅ keṟuḻttōṇ maṟavart tammiṉ
       kalliṭit tiyaṟṟiya viṭṭuvāyk kiṭaṅki         730
       ṉalleyi luḻanta celvart tammiṉ
       kollēṟṟup paintōl cīvātu pōrtta
       mākkaṇ muraca mōvila kaṟaṅka
       verinimin taṉṉa tāṉai nāppaṭ
       perunal yāṉai pōrkkaḷat toḻiya         735
       viḻumiya vīḻnta kuricilart tammiṉ
       puraiyōrkkut toṭutta polampūn tumpai
       nīryā reṉṉātu muṟaikarutupu cūṭṭik
       kāḻmaṇ ṭeḵkamoṭu kaṇaiyalaik kalaṅkip
       piripiṇai yarinta niṟañcitai kavayattu         740
       vāṉat taṉṉa vaḷanakar poṟpa
       nōṉkuṟaṭ ṭaṉṉa vūṉcāy mārpi
       ṉuyarnta vutavi yūkkalart tammi
       ṉivanta yāṉaik kaṇaniṟai kavarnta
       pularnta cāntiṉ viravuppūn teriyaṟ         745
       peruñce yāṭavart tammiṉ piṟarum
       yāvarum varuka vēṉōrun tammeṉa
       varaiyā vāyiṟ ceṟāa tiruntu
       pāṇar varuka pāṭṭiyar varukayāṇarp
       pulavaroṭu vayiriyar varukeṉa         750
       viruṅkiḷai purakku miravalark kellāṅ
       koṭuñci neṭuntēr kaḷiṟṟoṭum vīcik
       kaḷantōṟuṅ kaḷḷarippa
       marantōṟu maivīḻppa
       niṇavūṉcuṭ ṭurukkamaiya         755
       neykaṉintu vaṟaiyārppak
       kurūukkuyppukai maḻaimaṅkuliṟ
       parantutōṉṟā viyaṉakarāṟ
       palcālai mutukuṭumiyi
       ṉalvēḷvit tuṟaipōkiya         760
       tollāṇai nallāciriyar
       puṇarkūṭ ṭuṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappi
       ṉilantaru tiruvi ṉeṭiyōṉ pōla
       viyappuñ cālpuñ cemmai cāṉṟōr
       palarvāyp pukaraṟu ciṟappiṟ ṟōṉṟi         765
       yariyatantu kuṭiyakaṟṟip
       periyakaṟ ṟicaiviḷakki
       munnīr nāppaṇ ñāyiṟu pōlavum
       paṉmī ṉaṭuvaṭ ṭiṅkaḷ pōlavum
       pūtta cuṟṟamoṭu polintiṉitu viḷaṅkip         770
       poyyā nallicai niṟutta puṉaitārp
       perumpeyar māṟaṉ ṟalaiva ṉākak
       kaṭantaṭu vāyvā ḷiḷampal kōca
       riyaṉeṟi marapiṉiṉ vāymoḻi kēṭpap
       polampū ṇaiva ruṭpaṭap pukaḻnta         775
       maṟamiku ciṟappiṟ kuṟunila maṉṉa
       ravarum piṟarun tuvaṉṟip
       poṟpuviḷaṅku pukaḻavai niṟpukaḻn tētta
       vilaṅkiḻai makaḷir polaṅkalat tēntiya
       maṇaṅkamaḻ tēṟaṉ maṭuppa nāḷu         780
       makiḻntiṉi tuṟaimati peruma
       varaintunī peṟṟa nallū ḻiyaiyē.

                               itaṉ poruḷ

   ippāṭṭiṟku māṅkuṭimarutaṉār maturaikkāñciyeṉṟu tuṟaip peyarāṉaṉṟit
   tiṇaippeyarāṟ peyar kūṟiṉār. ittiṇaip peyar paṉṉirupaṭala mutaliya
   nūlkaḷāṟ kūṟiya tiṇaippeyaraṉṟu, tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya tiṇaippeyarp
   poruḷē ippāṭṭiṟkup poruḷākak kōṭaliṉ. vañci mēṟcellalāṉum, kāñci
   eñcātetir ceṉṟūṉṟalāṉum, "vañciyuṅ kāñciyun tammuṇ māṟē" eṉap
   paṉṉirupaṭalattiṟkūṟiya tiṇaippeyar ippāṭṭiṟkup poruḷaṉmaiyuṇarka. avar
   mutumoḻikkāñci mutaliyavaṟṟaip potuviyaleṉṟu oru paṭalamākkik kūṟaliṉ,
   avai tiṇaippeyarākāmai yuṇarka.
   iṉi, maturaikkāñciyeṉṟataṟku maturaiyiṭattu aracaṟkuk kūṟiya kāñciyeṉa
   virikka. iḵtu urupumporuḷum uṭaṉṟokkatu. "kāñci tāṉē peruntiṇaip
   puṟaṉē" (tol. puṟat. cū 22) eṉpataṉāṟ kāñci peruntiṇaikkup puṟaṉāyiṟṟu.
   atu, "pāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ pallāṟṟāṉu, nillā vulakam pulliya neṟittē"
   (tol. puṟat. cū. 23) eṉpatām. itaṉporuḷ : pāṅku aruciṟappiṉ-taṉakkut
   tuṇaiyillāta vīṭupēṟu nimittamāka, pala āṟṟāṉum-aṟam poruḷ iṉpameṉṉum
   poruṭpakutiyāṉum, avaṟṟu uṭpakutiyākiya uyirum yākkaiyum celvamum
   iḷamaiyu mutaliyavaṟṟāṉum, nillā ulakam pulliya neṟittu-nilaipēṟillāta
   ulakiyaṟkaiyaip poruntiya naṉṉeṟiyiṉai uṭaittu akkāñciyeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē,
   vīṭupēṟu nimittamākap palvēṟu nilaiyāmai cāṉṟōraṟaiyuṅ kuṟippiṉatu
   kāñcittiṇaiyeṉpatu poruḷāyiṟṟu.
   icceyyuṭpulavar ipporuḷē kōṭaliṉ, yām ipporuḷē tara urai kūṟukiṉṟām
   1-2. [ōṅkutirai viyaṉparappi, ṉolimunnīr varampāka :] viyalparappiṉ
   ōṅku tirai oli munnīr varampu āka - akalattaiyuṭaittākiya
   nīrpparappiṉkaṇṇē ōṅkutirai olikkuṅkaṭal ellaiyāka,
   3-4. tēṉ tūṅkum uyar cimaiyam malai nāṟiya viyal ñālattu-tēṉiṟāl tūṅkum
   uyarnta ucciyaiyuṭaiya malaikaḷ tōṉṟiya akalattaiyuṭaiya ñālattiṉ
   kaṇṇē,
   5. valam mātirattāṉ vaḷi koṭpa-valamāka ākāyattiṉ kaṇṇē kāṟṟuc cuḻala,
   6. viyal nāṇmīṉ neṟi oḻuka-akalattaiyuṭaiya nāṇmīṉkaḷ tām naṭakkum
   neṟiyiṉkaṇṇē naṭakka,
   7, pakal ceyyum ce ñāyiṟum-pakaṟpoḻutai uṇṭākkum civanta pakalōṉum,
   8. iravu ceyyum veḷ tiṅkaḷum-irāppoḻutai uṇṭākkum veḷḷiya matiyum,
   9. mai tīrntu kiḷarntu viḷaṅka-kuṟṟamaṟṟut tōṉṟiviḷaṅka,
   10. maḻai toḻil utava-mēkam tāṉē peytaṟṟoḻilai vēṇṭuṅkālattē tara,
   @uḻavuttoḻiṟku utavaveṉṟumām.
   mātiram koḻukka-ticaikaḷellām taḻaippa,
   malaikaḷ viḷaintukoḻukka veṉṟumām.
   11. [toṭuppi ṉāyiram vittiyatu viḷaiya :] toṭuppiṉ vittiyatu āyiram
   viḷaiya-oruvitaippiṉkaṇṇē vitaittavitai āyiramāka viḷaiya,
   12. nilaṉum-viḷainalaṅkaḷum,
   maraṉum-maraṅkaḷum,
   payaṉ etirpu tanta-palluyirkkum tampayaṉkoṭuttalai #ēṟaṭṭukkoṇṭu
   taḻaippa,
   13. nōy ikantu &nōkku viḷaṅka-makkaṭkup $paciyum piṇiyum nīṅki aḻaku
   viḷaṅka,
   14-5. [mētaka mikappolinta, vōṅkunilai vayakkaḷiṟu :] mika polinta ōṅku
   nilai vaya kaḷiṟu mē taka-aṟavum polivupeṟṟa %ulakattait tāṅkutal
   vaḷarun taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya valiyaiyuṭaiyavākiya tikkayaṅkaḷ ivar
   tāṅkutaliṉ varuttamaṟṟu mēmpāṭutaka,

   tuṇaṅkaikkūttiṉ cīrkku,
   27. piṇai yūpam eḻuntu āṭa-ceṟinta kuṟaittalaippiṇam eḻuntu niṉṟu
   āṭukaiyiṉālē,
   28. añcu vanta pōrkkaḷattāṉ-4añcutaluṇṭāṉa pōrkkaḷattiṭattē,
   29. āṇ 5talai aṇaṅku aṭuppiṉ-āṇmakkaḷ talaiyākiya nōkkiṉārai varuttum
   aṭuppiṉkaṇṇē,
   --------
   1 "iyalpuḷik kōlōccu maṉṉava ṉāṭṭa, peyalum viḷaiyuḷuntokku" (kuṟaḷ,
       545) ; "kōṉilai tirintiṭiṟ kōṇilai tiriyum, kōṇilai tirintiṭiṉ
       mārivaṟaṅ kūrum" (maṇi. 7:8-9) ; "kōṇilai tirintu nāḻi kuṟaipaṭap
       pakalkaṇ miñci, nīṇila māriyiṉṟi viḷaivaḵkip paciyu nīṭip, pūṇmulai
       makaḷir poṟpiṟ kaṟpaḻintaṟaṅkaṇ māṟi, yāṇaiyiv vulaku kēṭā
       maracukōl kōṭi ṉeṉṟāṉ" (cīvaka. 255)
       2 kuḻumpu : ikkālattuk kumpu eṉa maruvivaḻaṅkum.
       3 tuṇaṅkaiyiṉ iyalpai, muruku. 56-ām aṭiyiṉ uraiyiṉāl aṟiyalākum.
       4 pēy āṭiyayarum iṭaṅkaḷ accam taruvaṉavātalai maṇimēkalaiyil,
       "kaṇṭoṭ ṭuṇṭu kavaiyaṭi peyarttut, taṇṭāk kaḷippiṉāṭuṅ kūttuk,
       kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīik kaṭunavai yeyti, viṇṭōr ticaiyiṉ viḷittaṉaṉ
       ............... pēykkeṉ ṉuyirkoṭut tēṉeṉa" (6:125-30) eṉavarum
       pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭuñ ceytiyālaṟiyalākum.
       5 talaiyaip pēykaḷ aṭuppākak kōṭal ; "arakkar muṭikaḷaiyaṭuppu
       vakirvaṉa", "muṭittalai yoppa vaṭuppeṉa vaittu" (tiruvakuppu,
       porukaḷat, cerukkaḷat.)

   ---------
   30-31. vaya vēntar oḷ kuruti ciṉam tīyiṉ peyarpu poṅka-valiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   vēntaruṭaiya oḷḷiya 1kurutiyākiya ulai ciṉamākiya tīyiṉ maṟukip
   poṅkukaiyiṉālē,
   32-8. [teṟalaruṅ kaṭuntuppiṉ, viṟalviḷaṅkiya viḻuccūrppiṟ, ṟoṭittōṭkai
   tuṭuppāka, vāṭuṟṟa vūṉcōṟu, neṟiyaṟinta kaṭivāluva, ṉaṭiyotuṅkip
   piṟpeyarāp, paṭaiyōrkku murukayara :]
   toṭi tōḷ 2kai tuṭuppu āka (34) āṭu uṟṟa ūṉ cōṟu (35) 3vīravaḷaiyai
   uṭaiyavākiya tōḷaiyuṭaiya kaikaḷ tuṭuppākak koṇṭu tuḻāvi aṭutaluṟṟa
   ūṉiṉākiya cōṟṟai,
   neṟi aṟinta kaṭi vāluvaṉ-iṭumuṟaimaiyaṟinta pēy 4maṭaiyaṉ.
   teṟal aru kaṭu tuppiṉ (32) viṟal. viḷaṅkiya viḻu cūrppiṉ (33) aṭi
   otuṅki piṉ peyarā (37) paṭaiyōrkku muruku ayara (38)-pakaivarāṟ
   kōpittaṟku ariya kaṭiya valiyiṉaiyum veṟṟiviḷaṅkiya cīriya
   koṭuntoḻiliṉaiyumuṭaiyarāy iṭṭa aṭivāṅkip piṉpōkāta vīrarkku
   vēḷviceyyumpaṭi,
   39. amar kaṭakkum viyal tāṉai-pōraivellum akalattaiyuṭaiya
   paṭaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya,
   pōrkkaḷattē (28) āṭuṟṟacōṟṟai (35) vāluvaṉ (36) aṭipeyarā (37) vīrarkku
   murukayarak (38) kaṭakkuntāṉaiyeṉka.
   40-42. [teṉṉavaṟ peyariya tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ, ṟoṉmutu kaṭavuṭ piṉṉar mēya,
   varaittā ḻaruvip poruppiṟ poruna :]
   varai tāḻ aruvi poruppiṉ kaṭavuḷ-pakka malaiyilē viḻukiṉṟa
   aruviyiṉaiyuṭaiya potiyiṉmalaiyilirukkum kaṭavuḷ,
   teṉṉavaṉ peyariya tuṉ aru tuppiṉ tol mutu kaṭavuḷ piṉṉar mēya
   poruna-5irāvaṇaṉait tamiḻnāṭṭaiyāḷātapaṭi
   ------
   1 kurutiyai ulaiyākap peytal, "avaṟṟi ṉulaiyeṉa virattam viṭuvaṉa"
       (porukaḷattalakai vakuppu.)
       2 kaikaḷait tuṭuppākakkoḷḷal : "avarkaravakappai yavaikoṭu pukaṭṭi
       yaṭuvaṉa" (porukaḷattalakai vakuppu)
       3 toṭi-vīravaḷai ; maturaik. 720, na.
       4 maṭaiyaṉ-cōṟākkuvōṉ.
       5 teṉṉāṭṭai āṇṭu kuṭikaḷait tuṉpuṟutti vanta irāvaṇaṉai, akattiyar
       potiyiṉmalai urukumpaṭi icaipāṭi ilaṅkaikkup pōkkiṉareṉpatu paṇṭai
       varalāṟu ; avar icaiyilē vallunareṉpatu, "maṉṉu makattiyaṉyāḻ
       vācippa" (tirukkailāya ñāṉavulā), "tamiḻkkuṉṟil vāḻuñ caṭātāri
       pēryāḻ taḻaṅkut tirukkai" (takka. 539) eṉpavaṟṟālum potiyil
       urukiyatu, "iṉiya paina pōkkiṉa kiṭṭutaṟkariya valiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
       paḻamaimutirnta akattiyaṉ piṉṉē eṇṇappaṭṭuc cāṉṟōṉāyiruttaṟku
       mēviṉa oppaṟṟavaṉē,

   -----------
   "ticainilaik kiḷaviyi ṉāa kunavum" (tol. ecca. cū. 53) eṉṟataṉāl,
   irāvaṇaṉ teṉṟicai yāṇṭamaipaṟṟit 1teṉṉavaṉeṉṟār ; ippeyar pāṇṭiyaṟkum
   2kūṟṟuvaṟkum ēṟṟuniṉṟāṟpōla. akattiyaṉait teṉṟicaiyuyarnta noymmaipōka
   iṟaivaṉukkuc cīroppa iruntā ṉeṉpatupaṟṟik kaṭavuḷeṉṟār. irāvaṇaṉāḷutal
   pāyiraccūttirattu 3uraiyāciriyar kūṭiya uraiyāṉumuṇarka.
   iṉi, "muttēr muṟuvalāy nāmaṇam pukkakkā, lippōḻtu pōḻteṉ ṟatuvāyppak
   kūṟiya, vakkaṭavuṇ maṟṟak kaṭavuḷ" (kalit. 93:11-3) eṉa 4iruṭikaḷaiyum
   kaṭavuḷeṉṟu kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum kāṇka.
   itaṉāl, 5akattiyaṉuṭaṉ talaiccaṅkattup pāṇṭiyaṉiruntu tamiḻārāynta
   ciṟappukkūṟiṉār.
   6 kūṟṟuvaṉaiyutaitta kaṭavuḷeṉṟu iṟaivaṉākki, avaṉpiṉ ṉareṉṟatu
   akattiyaṉaiyeṉṟu poruḷkūṟiṉ, iṟaivaṉukkut
   tamiḻiṉ potiyamāl varaipō licaikkurukātu" (cōṇacaila. 26) eṉpataṉālum,
   akattiyar ataṉaiyurukkiyatu, "malai, muṉṉāḷurukku muṉinikarvai"
   (veṅkaiyulā, 319) eṉpataṉālum, irāvaṇaṉai icaipāṭi aṭakkiyatu,
   "potiyiliṉkaṇ iruntu irāvaṇaṉaik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatarai
   āṇṭu iyaṅkāmai vilakki" (tol. pāyiram. na.), "icaikkurukap
   paṭucilai-irāvaṇaṉaippiṇikkak kuṟumuṉipāṭum icaikku urukappaṭṭa
   potiyiṉmalai" (tañcai. 345, urai) eṉpavaṟṟālum uṇarappaṭum.
   ----------
   1 "teṉṉavaṉ malaiyeṭukkac cēyiḻai naṭuṅkak kaṇṭu" (tē. tirunā.
       tirukkaccimēṟṟaḷi)
       2 teṉṉavaṉeṉṉum peyar kūṟṟuvaṉukkātal "caṭṭat teṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉkaṭā
       vēntaṉai, veṭṭi" (takka. 684) ; "kālat teṉṉavaṉ makiṭamēṟāṉ"
       aruṇācala. pākampeṟṟa. 4 ; periyāḻ. 4.5:7.
       3 uraiyāciriyareṉṉum peyarkkuriyarāka ippoḻutu karutappaṭum
       iḷampūraṇar uraiyil icceyti kāṇappaṭāmai ārāyccikkuriyatu.
       4 iruṭikaḷaik kaṭavuḷeṉṟal : cilap. 11:5. cīvaka. 96, 1124 ; peruṅ.
       2. 11:150, 4. 10:153.
       5 icceytiyai iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ mutaṟcūttira uraiyiṉālaṟiyalākum.
       6 "maṟaimutu mutalvaṉ piṉṉar" (cilap. 12) eṉpataṟku, maṟaimutu
       mutalvaṉ-mātēvaṉ ;

   ------
   ivaṉ piṉṉar akat tampiyeṉṟal cālāmaiyāṉum, 1apporuṭaruṅkālattu,
   muṉṉavaṉ piṉṉavaṉ, muṉṉōṉ piṉṉōṉeṉṟallatu accol nillāmaiyāṉum atu
   poruntātu. "muṉṉarē cānāṇ muṉitakka mūppuḷa, piṉṉarum pīṭaḻikku nōyuḷa"
   (nālaṭi. 92) eṉap piṟāṇṭum muṉṉar piṉṉareṉpaṉa iṭamuṇarttiyē
   niṟkumeṉṟuṇarka.
   2porunaṉeṉṟatu tāṉ 3piṟarkku uvamikkappaṭuvāṉeṉṉum poruṭṭu.
   43. viḻu cūḻiya-cīriya mukapaṭāttaiyuṭaiyavāy,
   viḷaṅku ōṭaiya-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa paṭṭattaiyuṭaiyavāy,
   44. kaṭu ciṉatta-kaṭiya ciṉattaiyuṭaiyavāy,
   44-5. kamaḻ kaṭāttu aḷaṟu paṭṭa naṟu ceṉṉiya-kamaḻukiṉṟa matattālē
   cēṟuṇṭāṉa naṟiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyavāy,
   46. varai maruḷum uyar tōṉṟala-varaiyeṉṟumaruḷum uyarnta
   tōṟṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyavāy,
   47. viṉai naviṉṟa pēr yāṉai-pōrttoḻililē payiṉṟa periya yāṉai,
   icceyteṉeccakkuṟipparakiya viṉaiyeccamuṟṟukkaḷ aṭukki vantaṉavaṟṟai
   naviṉṟaveṉṉum peyareccattōṭu muṭikka.
   48. ciṉam ciṟantu kaḷaṉ uḻakkavum-ciṉamikkuk kaḷattuvīraraik
   koṉṟutiriyavum,
   49-50. mā eṭutta mali kurūu tukaḷ akal vāṉattu veyil
   karappavum-kutiraittiraḷ pakaivarmēṟ cellutalāluṇṭāṉa mikka
   niṟattaiyuṭaiyavākiya tūḷi akalutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ākāyattiṭattē
   veyilaimaṟaikkavum,
   51-2. vām pariya kaṭu tiṇ tēr kāṟṟu eṉṉa kaṭitu koṭpavum-tāvum
   kutiraikaḷaiyuṭaiya celavukaṭiya tiṇṇiyatēr kāṟṟuc cuṟṟiyaṭittateṉṉak
   kaṭitākac cuḻalavum,
   tiyaṉākiya kaṭavuḷ' eṉṟeḻutiya arumpata uraiyāciriyaratu urai, iṅkē
   kūṟappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟōṭu muraṇutal kāṇka.
   ----------
   1 "piṉṉar-piṉṉōṉ" (cilap. 12) eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallāruraiyai ōrka.
       2 "poruna-uvamikkappaṭuvāy" (muruku. 276, na.),
       "porunāku-ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟku oppuc collappaṭum nāku" (kalit. 109:4)
       eṉap piṟāṇṭum itaṉai yottē ivvuraiyāciriyar poruḷeḻututal
       aṟitaṟkuriyatu.
       3 "piṟarkkunī vāyi ṉallatu niṉakkup, piṟaruvama mākā voru peru
       vēntē" (patiṟ. 73:2-3) ; "piṟarkkuvaman tāṉallatu, taṉakkuvamam
       piṟarilleṉa" (puṟanā. 377:10-11)

   --------
   53-4. [vāṇmiku maṟamaintar, tōṇmuṟaiyāṉ vīṟumuṟṟavum :] vāḷ miku maṟam
   maintar tōḷ vīṟu muṟaiyāṉ muṟṟavum-vāṭpōrāṉ mikukiṉṟa maṟattaiyuṭaiya
   vīraruṭaiya tōḷ veṟṟi muṟaiyāka veṭṭutalāṉ muṟṟuppeṟavum,
   "viruntāyiṉai yeṟinīyeṉa viraimārpakaṅ koṭuttāṟ, karumpūṇaṟa
   veṟintāṅkava ṉiṉatūḻiṉi yeṉavē" (cīvaka. 2265) eṉṟār piṟarum.
   55-6. iru peru vēntaroṭu vēḷir cāya porutu avarai ceru
   veṉṟum-cēraṉcōḻaṉākiya iruvarākiya periya aracaruṭaṉē 1kuṟunila
   maṉṉarum iḷaikkumpaṭi porutu avaraip pōraiveṉṟum amaiyāmal,
   ummai : ciṟappummai.
   57-9. [ilaṅkaruviya varainīntic, curampōḻnta vikalāṟṟa, luyarntōṅkiya
   viḻucciṟappiṉ :]
   uyarntu ilaṅku aruviya varai nīnti ōṅkiya viḻu ciṟappiṉ-uyarntu
   viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aruvikaḷaiyuṭaiya malaiyiṟ kuṟiñcinilamaṉṉaraiyellām
   kavaṉṟu uyarnta cīriya talaimaiyiṉālē,
   ikal āṟṟal curam pōḻnta ciṟappiṉ - māṟupāṭṭai naṭattutalaiyuṭaiya
   pakaivarkāṭukaḷaip palavaḻikākappiḷanta ciṟappiṉālē,
   60. nilam tanta peru utavi-nāṭṭilirukkiṉṟa aracar nilaṅkaḷaiyellāṅ
   koṇṭa periya utaviyaiyum,
   2malaiyum kāṭum araṇāka irunta aracarai aḻittavaliyālē nāṭṭilirukkiṉṟa
   aracar tattam nāṭukaḷaiviṭṭāreṉṟatām.
   61. polantār mārpiṉ neṭiyōṉ umpal-poṉṉāṟ ceyta tāraiyaṇinta
   mārpiṉaiyumuṭaiya 3vaṭimpalampaniṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻiyil vantōṉē,
   ---------
   1 kuṟunilamaṉṉar-titiyaṉ mutalāyiṉōr ; maturaik. 128-9-ām aṭikaḷiṉ
       uraiyaip pārkka.
       2 "uṇṭākiya vuyarmaṇṇum, ceṉṟupaṭṭa viḻukkalaṉum, peṟalkūṭumiva
       ṉeñcuṟappeṟiṉ" (puṟanā. 17:24-6) eṉpataṉ karuttu ivvicēṭavuraiyuṭaṉ
       oppunōkkaṟpālatu.
       3 'munnīrkkaṇ vaṭimpalam niṉṟāṉeṉṟa viyappāl, neṭiyōṉeṉṟāreṉpa'
       (puṟanā. 9:10, urai), "aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti, vaṭivē
       leṟinta vāṉpakai poṟātu .................... kaṭal koḷḷa" (cilap.
       11:17-20), "āḻi vaṭimpalampa niṉṟāṉum" (naḷa. cuyam. 137),
       "kaṭalvaṭim palampa niṉṟa kaitavaṉ" (vi. pā. 15-ām pōr. 18)
       eṉpaṉavum, "maṉṉarpirāṉ vaḻutiyarkōṉ yāṉai (47) kaḷaṉuḻakkavum (48)
       tukaḷ (49) veyilkarappavum (50) tēr (51) koṭpavum (52) maintar (53)
       muṟṟavum (54) porutu ceruveṉṟum amaiyāmal (56) viḻucciṟappiṉ (59)
       nilantanta utaviyiṉaiyum (60) mārpiṉaiyumuṭaiya neṭiyōṉ (61) eṉka.

   ----------
   62-3. maram tiṉṉūu varai utirkkum narai urumiṉ ēṟu aṉaiyai-maraṅkaḷaic
   cuṭṭu malaikaḷainīṟākkum perumaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya urumēṟṟai oppai,
   "karunarainallēṟu" (kuṟun. 317:1) eṉṟār piṟarum. iṉ : acai.
   64. aru kuḻu miḷai-pakaivar cērtaṟkariya tiraṭciyaiyuṭaiya
   kāvaṟkāṭṭiṉaiyum,
   kuṇṭu kiṭaṅkiṉ-āḻattiṉaiyuṭaiya kiṭaṅkiṉaiyum,
   65. uyarntu ōṅkiya nirai putaviṉ-uyarntuvaḷarnta kōpuraṅkaḷiṭattu
   vāyilkaḷaiyum,
   nirai : ākupeyar. uyarcci ōṅkutalai vicēṭittuniṉṟatu.
   66. neṭumatil-neṭiya matiliṉaiyum,
   nirai ñāyil-niṟainta cūṭṭiṉaiyumuṭaiya,
   aḵtu eytāl maṟaitaṟku uyarappaṭuppatu.
   67. [ampumi ḻayilaruppam :] ayil ampu umiḻ aruppam-kūrmaiyaiyuṭaiya
   ampukaḷaiyumiḻum araṇkaḷai,
   miḷai (64) mutaliyavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya aruppameṉka.
   68-9. taṇṭātu talai ceṉṟu koṇṭu nīṅkiya viḻu ciṟappiṉ-amaiyāmal
   avviṭaṅkaḷilē ceṉṟu kaikkoṇṭu avviṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum pōṉa cīriya
   talaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya koṟṟavar (74),
   70-71. [teṉkumari vaṭaperuṅkal, kuṇakuṭakaṭa lāvellai :] teṉkumari
   ellai ā vaṭa peru kal ellai ā kuṇa kuṭa kaṭal ellai ā-teṉṟicaikkuk
   kumari ellaiyāka vaṭaticaikkup periyamēru ellaiyākak kīḻtticaikkum
   mēṟṟicaikkum kaṭal ellaiyāka iṭaiyil vāḻvōrellām,
   vaṭimpalampa niṉṟaruḷi" (parāntakatēvarmeyk.) eṉpatum, ippeyarāṟ
   peṟappaṭum ceytiyaik kūṟuvatukāṇka ; "veṉṟipaṭac
   civanteṟiyakkaruṅkaṭalvev valitolaintu, muṉṟeṟumam puṭaintu
   paṅkappaṭṭalaṟi mōtiviḻā, niṉṟapaṭi vaṭimpalampa niṉṟatukaṇ ṭēṉōru,
   miṉṟitukaṇ ṭaṉamputumai yeṉampayamviṭ ṭeḻattutittār", "mālaimuṭi
   yōṉvēlai vaṭimpalampa niṉṟataṉāl, vēlai vaṭimpalampaniṉṟā ṉeṉṟeṅkum
   viḷaṅkiyatāl" eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl, icciṟappup peyaraiyuṭaiyār ukkira kumāra
   pāṇṭiyareṉṟu kūṟuvār ; tiruvāla. 21:6, 7
   72. toṉṟu moḻintu toḻil kēṭpa-tammuṭaṉ paḻamaiyaiccolli ēval
   kēṭkumpaṭi,
   73. veṟṟamoṭu veṟuttu oḻukiya-veṟṟiyōṭē ceṟintu naṭanta,
   74. koṟṟavartam kōṉ ākuvai-veṟṟiṭaiyōr tammuṭaiya talaivaṉāṉa
   taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyai,
   75-6. vāṉ iyainta iru munnīr pēem nilaiiya iru pauvattu-mēkampaṭinta
   peritākiya 1mūṉṟu nīrmaiyaiyuṭaiya accam nilaipeṟṟa kariya kaṭaliṭattu,
   77-9. [koṭumpuṇari vilaṅkupōḻak, kaṭuṅkāloṭu karaicēra, neṭuṅkoṭimicai
   yitaiyeṭuttu :]
   kaṭu kāloṭu koṭu puṉari vilaṅku pōḻa itai eṭuttu-kaṭiya kāṟṟālē
   vaḷaikiṉṟa tiraiyaik kuṟukkē piḷantōṭumpaṭi pāyvirikkappaṭṭu,
   80. iṉṉicaiya muracam muḻaṅka-iṉiya ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya muracam
   muḻaṅkāniṟka,
   81-2. poṉ malinta viḻu paṇṭam nāṭu āra-poṉmikutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya
   cīriya carakkukaḷai nāṭṭiluḷḷār nukarumpaṭiyāka,
   [naṉkiḻitarum :] karai cēra (78) naṉku iḻitarum-karaiyaic cēra vāṇikam
   vāyttuvantu iḻitalaicceyyum,
   83. [āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy :] neṭu koṭi micai (79) āṭu iyal peru

   marakkalam,
   itaiyeṭuttu (79) muḻaṅka (80) āra iḻitarum (81) nāvāyeṉka.
   84-5. maḻai muṟṟiya malai puraiya tuṟai muṟṟiya tuḷaṅku
   irukkai-karumēkañcūḻnta malaipōlak kaṭalcūḻnta acaikiṉṟa iruppiṉaiyum,
   tuṟai : ākupeyar. carakkuppaṟittaṟkuk kaṭalil niṉṟamarakkalaṅkaḷ
   akkaṭal cūḻa niṉṟataṟku maḻaimuṟṟiya malai uvamamāyiṟṟu.
   nāvāy tuḷaṅkirukkaiyeṉka.
   86. teṉ kaṭal kuṇṭu akaḻi-teḷinta kaṭalākiya āḻattaiyuṭaiya
   kiṭaṅkiṉaiyumuṭaiya.
   87-8. cīr cāṉṟa uyar nelliṉ ūr koṇṭa uyar koṟṟava-talaimaiyamainta
   uyarnta nelliṉpeyaraippeṟṟa cāliyūraik koṇṭa uyarnta
   veṟṟiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉē,
   ---------
   1 mūṉṟu nīrmai-nilattaip paṭaittal, kāttal, aḻittal eṉpaṉa ; perumpāṇ.

   itaṉāl, taṉakkunaṭavātatōr ūrkoṇṭāṉeṉṟār.
   iṉṉai acaiyākki nellūreṉpārumuḷar.
   irukkaiyiṉaiyum (85) kiṭaṅkiṉaiyum (86) uṭaiya ūreṉka.
   89-92. [nīrttevvu niraittoḻuvar, pāṭucilampu micaiyēṟṟat, tōṭuvaḻaṅku
   makalāmpiyiṟ, kayaṉakaiya vayaṉiṟaikkum :]
   vayal akaiya niṟaikkum kayaṉ nīr (92) 1tevvum nirai 2tāḻuvar (89) pāṭu
   cilampum icai (90)-vayal taḻaikkumpaṭi nīrai niṟaittaṟkukkāraṇamāṉa

   olikkumōcai,
   ēṟṟat (90) tōṭu vaḻaṅkum akal āmpiyiṉ (91) icai (90) -ēṟṟattuṭaṉē
   ulāvum akaṉṟa paṉṟippattariṉ ōcai,
   93. mel toṭai val kiḻāar icai (95)-metteṉṟa kaṭṭukkaḷaiyuṭaiya valiya
   4pūṭṭaippoṟiyiṉōcai.
   94. atari koḷpavar icai (95)-kaṭāviṭukiṉṟavar ōcai,
   pakaṭu pūṇ teḷ maṇi icai (95)-erutukaḷ pūṇṭa teḷḷiya maṇiyiṉ ōcai,
   95. iru puḷ oppum icaiyē-payirkaḷiṟ periya kiḷi mutaliyavaṟṟai ōṭṭum
   ōcai,
   ēkāram : īṟṟacai.
   eṉṟum-ennāḷum,
   96-7. maṇi pū muṇṭakattu maṇal mali kāṉal paratavar makaḷir kuravaiyoṭu
   olippa-nīlamaṇipōlum pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiyavākiya kaḻimuḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya
   maṇaṟkuṉṟukaḷ mikka kaṭaṟkaraiyilirukkum parata
   -------------
   1 'tevvu' eṉṟapāṭamē nērāṉatu : "eṭuttal paṭuttal" (pirayōka. 40)

       srīkacciyappamuṉivarum ; kāñci. nakarēṟṟu. 142.

       pāle ḻuntatu virikati rulakelām parappi" (cīkāḷatti. kaṉṉiyar. 119)

   varuṭaiya makaḷirāṭum kuravaikkūttiṉ ōcaiyōṭēkūṭi āravāriyā niṟpa,

   irupeyarkku āṭum māṭumeṉpārumuḷar.

   108. [varumvaikaṉ mīṉpiṟaḻiṉum :] vaikal varum mīṉ piṟaḻiṉum-tāṉ

   117. iru karu ceṟuviṉ veḷ uppu pakarnaroṭu - periyakaḻiyiṭattu

   118-24. [oliyōvāk kaliyāṇar, mutuveḷḷilai mīkkūṟum, viyaṉmēval
   viḻuccelvat, tiruvakaiyā ṉicaicāṉṟa, ciṟukuṭip peruntoḻuvar,

   ciṟu kuṭi peru toḻuvar (122) oli ōvā kali yāṇar (118) mutuveḷḷilai
   (119)-ciṟiya kuṭikaḷāyp periya toḻilkaḷaic ceyvāruṭaiya āravāramum

   yeṉṉumūr,
   itu kuṟunilamaṉṉariruppu.
   pakarnaroliyōṭē (117-8) nelliṉōtai (110) mutaliya oli eṉṟum (111) ōvāta
   mutuveḷḷilaiyeṉka.
   mī kūṟum (119) iruvakaiyāṉ (111) viyal mēval viḻu celvattu (120) icai
   cāṉṟa (121) kuṭi keḻīiya nāṉilavaroṭu (123)-ulakattut toḻilkaḷil
   mēlākaccellum uḻavu vāṇikameṉkiṉṟa iraṇṭukūṟṟālē akalam
   poruntutalaiyuṭaiya cīriya celvattālē pukaḻniṟainta kuṭimakkaḷ
   poruntiṉa nāṉkunilattu vāḻvāruṭaṉē,

   kēṭkumpaṭiyāka,
   125-7. kāl eṉṉa kaṭitu urāay nāṭu keṭa eri parappi ālaṅkāṉattu añcuvara
   iṟuttu-kāṟṟeṉṉumpaṭi kaṭitākapparantu ceṉṟu pakaivar nāṭukeṭumpaṭi
   neruppaipparappit 2talaiyālaṅkāṉa meṉkiṉṟa ūrilē pakaivarkku
   accantōṉṟumpaṭi 3viṭṭu,
   "eriparanteṭuttal" (tol. puṟat. cū. 8) eṉṉun tuṟai kūṟiṟṟu.

   1 aḷavar-neytaṉilamākkaḷuḷ oruvakaiyār ; uppaḷattiṟkuriya toḻil ceyvōr.
       2 ivvūr talaiyālaṅkāṭeṉṟu vaḻaṅkum ; itu tēvārampeṟṟa oru civatalam

   ceyyum ēṟāyavaṉē,
   itaṉāṟ paratavarait taṉakkup paṭaiyāka aṭippaṭuttiṉamai kūṟiṉār.
   tuppu (140) mutaliyavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya paratavareṉka.
   paratavar-teṉṟicaikaṭkuṟunilamaṉṉar. atu, "teṉparatavar miṭalcāya,
   vaṭuvaṭukar vāḷōṭṭiya" (puṟanā. 378:1-2) eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭāṉumuṇarka.
   145. ariya ellām eḷitiṉiṉ koṇṭu-piṟarkku ariya nukar poruḷkalellām

   197-8. uyar nilai ulakam amiḻtoṭu peṟiṉum poy cēṇ nīṅkiya vāy
   naṭpiṉaiyē-oru poyyālē uyarnta nilaiyaiyuṭaiya tēvarulakai avarnukarum

   meyyuṭaṉē naṭpucceytalaiyuṭaiyai ;

   ceytaṉaiyeṉka.
   199-201. muḻaṅku kaṭal ēṇi malar talai ulakamoṭu uyarnta tēettu

   vāṇaṉeṉṉum cūraṉ vaitta cīriya poruṭṭiraḷkaḷiṉaip peṟuvaiyāyiṉum piṟar
   kūṟumpaḻi namakku varuvatākaveṉṟu karutāy ;
   204-5. viḻu niti ītal uḷḷamoṭu icai vēṭkuvaiyē-cīritākiya poruḷaik
   koṭuttalaiyuṭaittākiya neñcuṭaṉē pukaḻai virumpuvai;
   206. aṉṉāy - attaṉmaiyai yuṭaiyāy,
   [niṉṉoṭu muṉṉilai yevaṉō :] muṉṉilai niṉṉoṭu evaṉō-aimpoṟikaḷukkum
   nukarappaṭuvaṉavāy muṉṉiṟkappaṭuvaṉavākiya innukar poruḷkaṭku niṉṉōṭu
   eṉṉa uṟavuṇṭu ;

       kulamuta lāya piṟaikkoḻuntē" (pāṇṭik.)

   207-8. [koṉṉoṉṟu kiḷakkuva laṭupō raṇṇal, kēṭṭiciṉ vāḻi keṭukaniṉ

   niṉ avalam keṭuka-niṉṉiṭattuṇṭākiya māyai iṉikkeṭuvatāka ;

   icai keṭātu nilaiiyar-cēṭpulamellāmma ceṉṟu viḷaṅkum niṉṉuṭaiya
   nallapukaḻ orukālamum keṭātē nilaipeṟuvatāka;
   210-16. [tavāpperukkat taṟāyāṇa, raḻittāṉāk koḻuntiṟṟi, yiḻintāṉāp
   palacoṉṟi, yuṇṭāṉāk kūrnaṟaviṟ, ṟiṉṟāṉa viṉavaika, ṉilaṉoṭuk kallā
   voṇpal veṟukkaip, payiṉṟa vaṟiyā vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar :]
   tavā perukkattu aṟā yāṇar (210) vaḷam keḻu tirunakar (216)-keṭāta
   perukkattiṉaiyuṭaiya nīṅkāta putuvaruvāyālē celvam poruntiṉa
   tirumakaḷaiyuṭaiya nakar,
   aḻittu āṉā koḻu tiṟṟi (211) tiṉṟu (214)-aḻikkappaṭṭu viruppu amaiyāta
   koḻuviya tacaiyaittiṉṟu,
   "āṭaḻikka" eṉpa.
   āṉā pala coṉṟi iḻittu (212) āṉā kūr naṟaviṉ uṇṭu (213)-viruppamaiyāta
   palavakaippaṭṭa cōṟṟait tīteṉṟukūṟik kaḷippamaiyāta mikka kaḷḷai uṇṭu,
   āṉā iṉam (214) pāṇar (219)-avviraṇṭilum viruppamaiyarata
   tiraṭciyaiyuṭaiya pāṇareṉka.
   tiṉṟu (214) uṇṭu (213) āṉā iṉap (214) pāṇar (219) eṉka.
   vaikal (214)-nāṭōṟum,
   nilaṉ eṭukkallā oḷ pal veṟukkai (215)-nilañcumakkamāṭṭāta oḷḷiya
   palavākiya poruṭṭiraḷkaḷaiyum,
   eṉṟatu pūṇkaḷaiyum poṉṉaiyum
   payaṉ aṟavu aṟiyā nakar (216)-ekkālamum payaṉkeṭutalaṟiyāta nakar.

   tammiṭattu neruṅkiṉakōtai tam kaṇavar mārpiṉ mālaiyuṭaṉē aḻakupeṟak
   kūṭi avarkaḷuṭaṉē nīrāṭum ōcai,
   266-7. aṉaittum akal iru vāṉattu imiḻntu iṉitu icaippa-avvōcai

   perumaiyaiyuṭaiya ākāyattēceṉṟu muḻaṅki āṇṭuvāḻvārkku iṉitāka olippa,
   268-9. kuruku narala maṉai marattāṉ mīṉ cīvum pāṇ cēriyoṭu-kurukeṉṉum

   pāṇarkuṭiyiruppiṟ pāṭalāṭalāl eḻunta ōcaiyōṭē,
   270. marutam cāṉṟa-ūṭalākiya uripporuḷamainta,
   taṇpaṇai cuṟṟi-marutanilañ cūḻappaṭṭu,
   orucār-orupakkam,

   (267) taṇpaṇai cuṟṟappaṭṭu (270) eṉka.

   3kavvai kaṟuppa-eḷḷiḷaṅkāy muṟṟa,

   273. āḻnta kuḻumpil tiru maṇi kiḷara-āḻntakuḻiyilē tiruviṉaiyuṭaiya

   274-6. [eḻunta kaṭaṟṟi ṉaṉpoṉ koḻippap, peruṅkaviṉ peṟṟa ciṟutalai

   278. pāayaṉṉa pāṟai aṇintu-parappiṉālotta pāṟai aḻakupeṟṟu,
   279-81. [nīlat taṉṉa paimpayir micaitoṟum, veḷḷiyaṉṉa voḷvī yutirntu,
   curimukiḻ mucuṇṭaiyoṭu mullai tāay :] nīlattu aṉṉa paimpayir micaitoṟum

   oḷḷiyapūkkaḷ utirntu parantu,
   282-4. [maṇimaru ṇeyta luṟaḻak kāmar, tuṇinīr mellavaṟṟoyyiloṭu malara,

   uṭaṉ koṇarnta puraviyoṭu-cērakkoṇṭuvanta kutiraikaḷōṭē,

   324. vaikal tōṟum vaḻi vaḻi ciṟappa-nāṭōṟum muṟaimaiyē muṟaimaiyē
   mikukaiyiṉālē vaḷampala payiṉṟu (325) eṉka.
   325. neytal cāṉṟa-iraṅkalākiya uripporuḷamainta,
   vaḷam pala payiṉṟu-pala celvamum neruṅkappaṭṭu,
   orucār (314), perunīrōccunar (321) kalaṉuymmār tēettuk (322) koṇarnta
   puraviyōṭē (323) muttam (315) vaḷai (316) kūlamāṉavai (317) aṉaittum
   (323) vaḻivaḻicciṟappa (324) vaḷampala payilappaṭṭu (325) eṉka.
   neytalcāṉṟa (335) parantōṅkuvaraippiṉ kaṇṇē (319) vaḷam pala
   payilappaṭṭu (325) eṉamuṭikka.

   1 "koḻumīṉuṇṭa vaṉṉaṅkaḷē" (tirucciṟ. 188) eṉpataṉ uraiyil,
       'koḻumīṉeṉpatu orucāti' (pēr.) eṉa eḻutiyiruttalāl ippeyaramainta
       oruvakai mīṉ uṇmai peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu ; "koḻumīṉ kuṟaiya votuṅki"
       (ciṟupāṇ. 41) ; "neyttalaik koḻumīṉ" "koḻumīṉ cuṭupukai" (naṟ.

   332-7. [kalaitāya vuyarcimaiyattu, mayilakavu malipoṅkar, mantiyāṭa

   koḻittu ēṟaṭṭa veḷḷiya talaiyiṉaiyuṭaittākiya tiraṭciyaiyuṭaiya
   maṇaṟkuṉṟiṭattu maṇattaiyuṭaiya poḻil,

       2 (pi-m.) 'iṉkaṇ'
       3 āṅkaṇeṉpataṟku ūriṭameṉṟu iṅku eḻutiyavāṟē, "akalāṅka ṇaḷaimāṟi"

       ikkaṭalilumuḷa ; avai paṉaimīṉeṉṟum yāṉaimīṉeṉṟum mōṅkileṉṟum
       aṉuviṣameṉṟum peyaruṭaiyaṉa" (takka. 384, urai) ; "uḷvaḷain tulāya
       ciṉṉai yoṇcuṟāp paṉaimī ṉūṟai, teḷviḷit tirukkai tanti timiṅkila
       mirintu pāynta" (kanta. kaṭalpāy. 11); immīṉ, paṉaiyaṉeṉṟum
       vaḻaṅkum ; "paṉaiyaṉṟēḷi" aruṇaikkalampakam, 45)

   2kālkoḷḷattoṭaṅkiya 3ēḻānāḷantiyilē tīrttamāṭutal marapu.
   nāṭu-avviḻaviṟkuttiraṇṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷār,
   ārttaṉṟē-ārttaāravāram,
   aṟṟēyeṉpatu aṉṟēye melintatākki uvamavurupākkalumoṉṟu

   1 "oḻukkattu nīttār" (kuṟaḷ, 21) eṉpataṉ uraiyiṟ parimēlaḻakar,
       'tamakkuriya oḻukkattiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu tuṟantār' eṉṟum, vicēṭa
       uraiyil, 'tamakku uriya oḻukkattiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu tuṟattalāvatu :
       tattam varuṇattiṟkum nilaikkum uriya oḻukkaṅkaḷai vaḻuvātoḻuka aṟam
       vaḷarum ; aṟam vaḷarap pāvantēyum ; pāvantēya aṟiyāmai nīṅkum ;
       aṟiyāmai nīṅka nitta anittaṅkaḷatu vēṟupāṭṭuṇarvum, aḻitaṉ
       mālaiyavāya immai maṟumaiyiṉpaṅkaḷiṉuvarppum, piṟavit tuṉpaṅkaḷum
       tōṉṟum ; avai tōṉṟa vīṭṭiṉkaṇ ācaiyuṇṭām ; aḵtuṇṭākap piṟavikkuk
       kāraṇamākiya yōka muyaṟciyuṇṭām; aḵtuṇṭāka meyyuṇarvu piṟantu
       puṟappaṟṟākiya eṉateṉpatum akappaṟṟākiya yāṉeṉpatum viṭum. ākalāṉ
       ivviraṇṭu paṟṟaiyum immuṟaiyē uvarttu viṭutaleṉakkoḷka' eṉṟum
       eḻutiyavai iṅkē aṟitaṟkuriyaṉa.

   paṉantāraiyuṭaiya cēraṉuṭaiya periyanāḷōlakka iruppilē,
   525-6. viḻumiyōr kuḻīi viḻaivu koḷ kampalai kaṭuppa kalleṉa (538)
   ellākkalaikaḷaiyumuṇarnta cīriyōr tiraṇṭu avaṉ
   kēṭpat1tarukkaṅkaḷaikkūṟi virumputalkoṇṭa āravārattaiyoppak kalleṉṟa
   ōcai naṭakka,
   palacamayattōrum tammiṟṟām māṟupaṭṭukkūṟun tarukkattaic cērak kūṟak
   kēṭṭirukkiṉṟa2kampalaipōlaveṉṟār.
   526. palavuṭaṉ-kūṟātaṉa palavuṟṟuṭaṉē,
   527. cēṟum nāṟṟamum palaviṉ cuḷaiyum-tēṉum nāṟṟamum uṭaiyavākiya
   palāppaḻattiṉ cuḷaiyum,
   cēṟeṉṟār, cuḷaiyilirukkiṉṟa tēṉai.
   528. vēṟu paṭa kaviṉiya 3tēm mā kaṉiyum-vaṭivu vēṟupaṭa aḻakupeṟṟa
   iṉiya māviṟpaḻaṅkaḷaiyum,
   'cēṟu nāṟṟamum vēṟupaṭak kaviṉiya, palaviṉ cuḷaiyun tēmāṅ kaṉiyum'
   eṉṟum pāṭam.
   529. pal vēṟu uruviṟ kāyum-palavāy vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭiviṉaiyuṭaiya pākaṟkāy
   vāḻaikkāy4vaḻutuṇaṅkāy mutaliyakāykaḷaiyum,
   paḻaṉum-vāḻaippaḻam muntirikaippaḻamutaliya paḻaṅkaḷaiyum,
   530-31. koṇṭal vaḷarppa koṭi viṭupu kaviṉi mel piṇi aviḻnta kuṟu muṟi
   aṭakum-maḻai paruvattē peytu vaḷarkkaiyiṉālē koṭikaḷviṭṭu aḻakupeṟṟu
   melliya curuḷvirinta ciṟiya ilaikaḷaiyuṭaiya ilaikkaṟikaḷaiyum,
   koṭiyeṉṟār, oḻuṅkupaṭa viṭukiṉṟa kiḷaikaḷai.
   532. [amirtiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa tīñcēṟṟuk kaṭikaiyum :] tī cēṟu amirtu iyaṉṟaṉṉa
   kaṭikaiyum-iṉiya pākiṉāṟkaṭṭiṉa amirtam tēṟākanīṇṭāṟpōṉṟa
   kaṇṭacarukkarait tēṟṟaiyum,
   533. pukaḻ paṭi paṇṇiya peru ūṉ cōṟum-pukaḻccikaḷ uṇṭākaccamaitta
   periya iṟaiccikaḷaiyuṭaiya cōṟṟaiyum,
   ----------
   1 cēraṉavaiyun tarukkamum : maṇi. 26:63-4.
       2 "kalviyiṟ ṟikaḻkaṇak kāyar kampalai" eṉṟār kamparum.
       3 tēmāveṉpatu māvil oru cātiyeṉpar.
       4 vaḻutuṇaṅkāy-kattarikkāy ; "vaḻutuṇaṅkāy vāṭṭum" (taṉip.)

   534. kīḻ cela 1vīḻnta kiḻaṅkoṭu-pār aḷavākavīḻnta kiḻaṅkuṭaṉē,
   534-5. [piṟavu, miṉcōṟu tarunar palvayi ṉukara :] pal vayiṉ nukara iṉ

   eṉṟatu, cōṟiṭuñcālaikaḷai.
   536-44. vālitai yeṭutta vaḷitaru vaṅkam, palvēṟu paṇṭamiḻitarum
   paṭṭiṉat, tolle ṉimiḻicai māṉak kalleṉa, naṉantalai viṉaiñar kalaṅkoṇṭu
   maṟukap, peruṅkaṭaṟ kuṭṭattup pulavuttiraiyōta, miruṅkaḻi maruvip pāyap
   periteḻun, turukeḻu pāṉāḷ varuvaṉa peyartaliṟ, palvēṟu puḷḷi ṉicaiyeḻun
   taṟṟē, yallaṅkāṭi yaḻitaru kampalai :]
   naṉa talai viṉaiñar kalam koṇṭu maṟuka (539)-akaṉṟa iṭattaiyuṭaiya

   vāl itai eṭutta vaḷi taru vaṅkam (536)-naṉṟākiya pāyviritta kāṟṟukkoṇṭu
   varukiṉṟa marakkalam,
   peru kaṭal kuṭṭattu pulavu tirai ōtam (540) iru kaḻi maruviya
   pāyaperitu eḻuntu (541) uru keḻu pālnāḷ varuvaṉa (542)-periya
   kaṭalcūḻnta iṭattiṉiṉṟum pulāṉāṟun tiraiyaiyuṭaiya ōtam kariya kaḻiyiṟ
   pukārmukattēyēṟip parakkiṉṟa aḷavilē mikkeḻuntu accamporuntiya
   naṭuviyāmattē varukiṉṟavaṟṟiṉ paṇṭam (537) eṉka.
   2 periteḻunteṉṟār, pāyumvāṅkic carakkumpaṟiyāmal varukiṉṟamai tōṉṟa
   ōtamaruvippāyaveṉṟār, avvōtavēṟṟattilē kaḻiyilē varumeṉpatuṇarttaṟku.
   pal vēṟu paṇṭam iḻitarum paṭṭiṉattu (537) olleṉ imiḻ icai māṉa

   545-6. [oṇcuṭa ruruppoḷi maḻuṅkac ciṉantaṇintu ceṉṟa ñāyiṟu :] oḷ cuṭar

   556-8. [neṭunaka, rellai yellā nōyoṭu pukuntu, kalleṉ mālai nīṅka :]

   kātaliṉṟuṇaipuṇartaṟkup (550) puṉaiyūut tuṇaippak (551) kuṭaiya (552)
   araippa maṟuka (553) maṭuppa (544) iravu varumpaṭi (549) ñāyiṟu (546)

   (557) piṉṉar nīṅka (558) muntaiyāmañceṉṟapiṉṟai (620) eṉakkūṭṭuka.
   558. nāṇu koḷa-puṇarccinīṅkiṟṟākat tamakku uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇai
   tammiṭattē taṭuttukkoḷḷa,
   559-61. [ēḻpuṇar ciṟappi ṉiṉṟoṭaic cīṟiyāḻ,2tāḻpayaṟ kaṉaikural
   kaṭuppap paṇṇuppeyarttu, vīḻtuṇai taḻīi.]
   ēḻ puṇar ciṟappiṉ iṉ toṭai ciṟu yāḻ paṇṇu
   peyarttu-icaiyēḻumtaṉṉiṭattēkūṭiṉa talaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉiya

   3pāṭṭuk kāmattaiviḷaivittaliṉ, yāḻaivācittu vīḻtuṇai taḻīi yeṉṟār.
   561-2. [viyalvicumpu kamaḻa nīrtiraṇ ṭaṉṉa kōtai piṟakkiṭṭu :] nīr

   569-72. [nuṇpū ṇākam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅki, māyap poypala kūṭṭik kavavuk

   cēyarum naṇiyarum nalaṉ nayantu vanta (571) iḷa pal celvar

   vañcaṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya poyvārttaikaḷālē mutaṟkūṭṭikkoṇṭu avaruṭaiya
   nuṇṇiya pūṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya mārpait tammārpilē vaṭuppaṭumpaṭiyāka muyaṅkip
   piṉṉar,
   kavavu karantu (570) vaḷam tapa vāṅki (572)-aṅṅaṉam1aṉpuṭaiyārpōlē

   celvamellām keṭumpaṭiyāka vāṅkikkoṇṭu,
   573-4. nuṇ tātu uṇṭu vaṟu pū tuṟakkum mel ciṟai vaṇṭu iṉam māṉa-pū

   itu toḻiluvamam.
   574-5. puṇarntōr neñcu ēmāppa iṉ tuyil tuṟantu-tammai nukarntōruṭaiya
   neñcu kalakkamuṟumpaṭi avariṭattu iṉiya kūṭṭattai nērākak kaiviṭṭu,
   576. paḻam tēr vāḻkkai paṟavai pōla-paḻumaramuḷḷa iṭantēṭic ceṉṟu,
   avaṟṟiṉ paḻattaiyē ārāyntu vāṅki nukartalait tamakkut toḻilākavuṭaiya
   puḷḷiṉampōla,
   tammuyiraip pātukākkiṉṟa vāḻkkaittoḻilai vāḻkkaiyeṉṟār.
   577-8. [koḻuṅkuṭic celvarum piṟaru mēeya, maṇampuṇarntōṅkiya vaṇaṅkuṭai
   nallil :]
   koḻu kuṭi celvarum piṟarum mēeya aṇaṅku uṭai nal il-vaḷaviya
   kuṭiyiṟpiṟanta celvarum avarkaḷāṟṟōṉṟiya piṟacelvarum mēvappaṭṭa
   illuṟai teyvaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya naṉṟākiya akaṅkaḷil,
   2kuṭiccelvareṉṟār, nāṉkuvaruṇattai ; piṟareṉṟār, avarkaḷāṟ ṟōṉṟiya
   ēṉaiccātikaḷai.
   578-9. maṇam puṇarntu ōṅkiya āy poṉ avir toṭi pacu iḻai
   makaḷir-varaintu koḷḷappaṭṭu uyarccipeṟṟa ōṭavaitta poṉṉāṟceyta
   viḷaṅkukiṉṟa toṭiyiṉaiyum paciyapūṇiṉaiyumuṭaiya makaḷir,
   580. oḷ cuṭar viḷakkattu-oḷḷiya viḷakkiṉuṭaiya oḷiyilē,
   palar uṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi-palaruñ cēraneruṅki,
   581-2. nīl niṟa vicumpil amarntaṉar āṭum vāṉavamakaḷir
   māṉa-nīlaniṟattaiyuṭaiya ākāyattē neñcamarntuviḷaiyāṭum teyva makaḷir
   varuttumāṟupōla,
   ---------
   1. "meypaṭu maṉpi ṉārpōla virumpiṉārk karuttun taṅkaḷ, poypaṭu miṉpam"
       (tiruviḷai. tirunakarap. 47)
       2. periya. tirukkuṟippu. 99-103 ; tiruviḷai. tirunakaraṅkaṇṭa. 45,
       pārkka.

   tāḻpayaṟ kaṉaikural kaṭuppa (560) eṉṟu muṉṉarvantataṉai ivviṭattuk
   kūṭṭuka.

   tuṟaiyiṭattuk kuvinta maṇalilē,
   muṉaii-veṟuttu,
   586-7. [meṉṟaḷirk. koḻuṅkompu koḻuti :] koḻukompu mel taḷir koḻuti

   587-8. [nīrnaṉai mēvara, neṭuntoṭark kuvaḷai vaṭimpuṟa vaṭaicci :]

   vaṭimpilē viḻumpaṭi uṭuttu,

   ceṉṟu poruḷ tarutaṟkuriya iḷaiyarōṭu viḷaiyāṭutalaicceyya,
   aṅṅaṉam viḷaiyāṭi vacikarittu uṟavukoṇṭu poruḷvāṅkutal avarkku iyalpu.
   vāṉavamakaḷirmāṉa (582) neñcunaṭukkuṟūuk koṇṭimakaḷir (583)
   palaruṭaṉṟuvaṉṟik (580) kuvavumaṇalilē (586) muḻaviṉ makiḻntaṉarāṭi
   (585) ataṉaimuṉaiik (586) kuvaḷaiyaiyaṭaiccik (588) kōtaiyai (562)

   koṇṭāṭukiṉṟa piṟantanāḷiṭattu,
   avaṉpeyarai annāḷ peṟutaliṉ, peyaraippeṟṟa naṉṉāḷeṉṟār.
   619. cēri viḻaviṉ ārppu eḻuntāṅku-cērikaḷiluḷḷār koṇṭāṭukiṉṟa viḻāvālē
   āravārameḻuntāṟpōla,
   620. muntai yāmam ceṉṟa piṉṟai-muṟpaṭṭacāmam naṭanta piṉpu,
   koṇṭimakaḷir (583) poytalayarap (589) pāciḻaimakaḷir (579)
   vīḻtuṇaitaḻīi (561) nāṇukkoḷa (558) maṟavar (596) makiḻciṟantu
   tiritarak (599) kaṭuñcūṉmakaḷir kaitoḻutu (609) maṭuppa orucār (610)
   neṭuvēṭ pēṇukaiyiṉālē (614) maṉṟutoṟuniṉṟa kuravaiyum cēritoṟuniṉṟa
   (615) uraiyum pāṭṭum āṭṭum viravukaiyiṉālē (616) vēṟuvēṟupaṭṭa kampalai
   (617) cēriviḻaviṉ ārppeḻuntāṅku (619) veṟikoḷpu mayaṅkappaṭṭu (617)
   muntaiyāmañ ceṉṟapiṉṟai (620) eṉakkūṭṭuka.
   orucār (610) viraii (616) eṉka.
   ------------
   1 naṉṉaṉ-oru kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ.
       2 cila piratikaḷil, 'perumpeyar naṉṉāḷ' eṉṟu iruntatu.

   --------
   621. paṇilam kali avintu aṭaṅka-caṅkukaḷ āravāramoḻintu aṭaṅkikkiṭakka,
   621-2. kāḻ cāyttu noṭai navil neṭu kaṭai aṭaittu-caṭṭakkālai vāṅkip
   paṇṭaṅkaḷukku vilaikūṟum neṭiyakaṭaiyaiyaṭaittu,
   622-3. maṭa matar oḷ iḻai makaḷir paḷḷi ayara-maṭappattiṉaiyum
   cerukkiṉaiyum oḷḷiya aṇikalaṅkaḷaiyumuṭaiya makaḷir tuyilutalaicceyya,
   624-6. [nalvari yiṟāal puraiyu mellaṭai, yayirurup puṟṟa vāṭamai
   vicayaṅ, kavavoṭu piṭitta vakaiyamai mōtakam :]
   vicayam āṭu amai nal vari iṟāal puraiyum mel aṭai-pākilē
   camaittalamainta nallavarikaḷaiyuṭaiya tēṉiṟālaiyokkum melliya
   aṭaiyiṉaiyum,
   kavavoṭu ayir piṭitta vakai amai uruppu uṟṟa mōtakam-paruppum
   tēṅkāyumākiya uḷḷīṭu (uḷḷē iṭappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷ) kaḷōṭē1kaṇṭa carukkarai
   kūṭṭippiṭitta vakuppamainta vemmai poruntiṉa appaṅkaḷaiyum,
   627. tī cēṟu 2kūviyar tūṅkuvaṉar uṟaṅka-iṉiya pākōṭu cērttukkaraitta
   māviṉaiyumuṭaiya appavāṇikarum avaṟṟōṭēyiruntu tūṅkuvaṉarāyuṟaṅka,
   628. viḻaviṉ āṭum vayiriyar maṭiya-tirunāḷiṉkaṇṇē kūttāṭuṅ kūttar
   ataṉaiyoḻintu tuyilkoḷḷa,
   629. pāṭu āṉṟu avinta paṉi kaṭal puraiya-oliniṟaintaṭaṅkiṉa
   kuḷirnta kaṭalaiyokka,
   630. pāyal vaḷarvōr kaṇ iṉitu maṭuppa-paṭukkaiyilē tuyilkoḷḷavōr kaṇ
   iṉitākat tuyilkoḷḷa,
   631. [pāṉāṭ koṇṭa kaṅku liṭaiyatu :]
   pāl nāḷ koṇṭa kaṅkul-patiṉaintānāḻikaiyai muṭivākak koṇṭa kaṅkul,
   632-3. [pēyu maṇaṅku muruvukoṇ ṭāykōṟ, kūṟṟakkoḵṟēr kaḻutoṭu koṭpa :]
   pēyum aṇaṅkum uruvu koṇṭu kaḻutoṭu koṭpa-pēykaḷum varuttun teyvaṅkaḷum
   vaṭivukoṇṭu kaḻutuṭaṉē cuḻaṉṟutiriya,
   kaḻutu-pēyil orucāti.
   āy kōl kūṟṟam kol tēr accam aṟiyātu ēmamākiya (652) maturai (699)
   yeṉakkūṭṭi, yākkai nilaiyāteṉṟaṟintu maṟumaikku vēṇṭuvaṉa ceytu
   koṇṭamaiyiṉ, aḻakiya kōlaiyuṭaiya kūṟṟattiṉ kolaikku añcāmaṟ
   kāvaluṇṭāyirukkiṉṟa maturai yeṉka.
   "karumpāṭṭik kaṭṭi ciṟukālaik koṇṭār, turumpeḻuntu vēṅkāṟ ṟuyarāṇ
   ṭuḻavār, varunti yuṭampiṉ payaṉkoṇṭār kūṟṟam, varuṅkāṟ pariva tilar"
   (nālaṭi. 35) eṉṟār piṟarum. koḵṟēr-kālacakkaram.

   1. kaṇṭacarukkarai-kaṟkaṇṭu; oruvakaic carukkaraiyumām.
       2. kūviyar-appavāṇikar; perumpāṇ. 377, na.

   634. iṭi piṭi mēentōl aṉṉa iruḷ cērpu - kariyapiṭiyiṉ kaṇṇēmēviṉa

   iruḷ - karuñcaṭṭaiyumām.
   635. kallum maraṉum tuṇikkum kūrmai nilaṉ akaḻ uḷiyar (641)-kallaiyum

   636. toṭalai vāḷar-tūkkiṭṭa vāḷiṉaiyuṭaiyarāy,
   toṭutōl aṭiyar-ceruppukkōtta aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiyarāy,
   637-41. [kuṟaṅkiṭaip patitta kūrnuṉaik kuṟumpiṭic, ciṟanta karumai
   nuṇviṉai nuṇaṅkaṟa, ṉiṟaṅkavarpu puṉainta nīlak kacciṉar, meṉṉū lēṇip
   paṉmāṇ cuṟṟiṉar, nilaṉaka ḻuḷiyar :]
   ciṟanta karumai nuṇ viṉai nuṇaṅku aṟal (638)-mikka karumai yiṉaiyuṭaiya
   nuṇṇiya toḻilkaḷaiyuṭaiya nuṇmai taṉṉiṭattē paṟṟutalaiyuṭaiyatu,
   eṉṟatu iṟaimuṭinta cēlaiyeṉṟavāṟu ; ākupeyar.
   kuṟaṅkiṭai patitta kūr nuṉai kuṟu piṭi (637)-accēlaikaṭṭiṉa maruṅkiṟ
   kuṟaṅkiṭait teriyāmaṟ kiṭakkumpaṭiyaḻuttiṉa kūriya
   muṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya1cilacoṭṭai,
   niṟam kavarpu puṉainta nīlam kacciṉar (639)-palaniṟaṅkaḷait taṉṉiṭattē
   kaikkoṇṭu kaiceyyappaṭṭa nīlaniṟattaiyuṭaiya kacciṉaiyuṭaiyarāy,
   eṉṟatu, kaṭṭiṉa cēlaiyiṭattē cerukit tuṭaiyuṭaṉē cērntu kiṭakkuñ
   coṭṭaiyiṉmēlē kaṭṭiṉa nīlakkacciṉareṉṟavāṟu. atu teriyāmal tuṭaiyuṭaṉē
   cērntukiṭattaliṟ kuṟaṅkiṭaip patittaveṉṟār.
   mel nūl ēṇi pal māṇ cuṟṟiṉar (640)-melliya nūlāṟ ceyta ēṇiyai araiyilē
   palavāy māṭcimaippaṭaccuṟṟiya cuṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiyarāy,
   matiṟṟalaiyilē uḷḷēviḻaveṟintāṟ kaipōla matilaippiṭittuk koḷḷumpaṭi
   irumpāṟcamaittataṉait talaiyilēyuṭaiya nūṟkayiṟṟai uḷḷē viḻaveṟintu
   ataṉaip puṟampēniṉṟu piṭittuk koṇṭu ammatilai ēṟuvārākaliṉ, ēṇiyeṉṟār.

   1.cila coṭṭai-oruvakai vaḷaivukkatti ; uṭaivāḷumām ; "coṭṭaivāḷ

   1kaḷavukāṇṭaṟkum kāttaṟkuṅkūṟiya nūlkaḷ pōvāreṉṟār.

   651. [kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkuṅ kaiyaṟu kaṅkulum :] iṭaiyatu (631) kaṭavuḷ
   vaḻaṅkum kai aṟu kaṅkul-iraṇṭāñ cāmattiṟkum nāṉ kāñ cāmattiṟkum
   naṭuviṭattatākiya teyvaṅkaḷulāvuñ ceyalaṟṟa kaṅkul,
   ummaiyai yāmamumeṉap piṉṉēkūṭṭuka.
   652-3. [acca maṟiyā tēma mākiya, maṟṟai yāmam pakaluṟak kaḻippi :]
   maṟṟaiyeṉpataṉai muntaiyāmañ ceṉṟapiṉṟai (620) yeṉpataṉ piṉṉum
   mūṉṟāñcāmattiṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka.
   accamaṟiyātēmamākiya eṉṟum, maṟṟaiyeṉṟum yāmameṉṟum pirittu muṉṉē
   kūṭṭuka.
   pakal uṟa kaḻippi-iṅṅaṉam pakuttaluṟumpaṭi pōkki,
   muntai yāmañ ceṉṟa piṉṟai (620) maṟṟaip (653) pāṉāṭkoṇṭa kaṅkul (631)
   yāmattaiyum maṟṟai (653) iṭaiyatākiya (631) kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkuṅ kaṅkul
   (651) yāmattaiyum (653) kaliyavintaṭaṅkap (612) paḷḷiyayara (623)
   uṟaṅka (627) vayiriyarmaṭiyap (628) pēyum aṇaṅkum uruvukoṇṭu (632)
   kaḻutoṭukoṭpa (633)ūr kāppāḷar (647) pulipōlak (643) kaṇmāṟāṭavar
   oṭukkam oṟṟukaiyiṉālē (642) kaṇṇarāyk koḷkaiyarāy (644) āṇmaiyarāyk
   (645) kaṇaiyarāy (647) araināḷamayamum (649) eḻuntu nayamvantu
   vaḻaṅkaliṟ (650) paṉikkaṭalpuraiyap (629) pāyalilvaḷarvōr
   kaṇiṉitumaṭuppap (630) pakaluṟakkaḻippi (653) eṉamuṭikka.
   iṅṅaṉam kaḻikkiṉṟatu, irāppoḻuteṉṟuṇarka.

   1. kaḷavu kāṇṭaṟ kuriyanūlaik karavaṭacāttirameṉpar takka yākapparaṇi

       2. nūl-ciṟpanūl ; "nūlaṟipulavōr-ciṟpanūlai yaṟinta taccar"

   cēvalkaḷum tamakkuriya pēṭaikaḷai yaḻaikka,
   aṇi mayil akava-aḻakiyamayilkaḷ pēṭaikaḷai yaḻaikka,

       2. "kāṭi koṇṭa kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam" (neṭunal. 134) ; "nalattakaip

       pūntukil" (cīvaka. 71) ; "kāṭi kalanta kōṭik kaliṅkam" (peruṅ. 1.
       54:9)
       3. "taṉiccēvakamāvatu varuvicaip puṉalaik kaṟciṟaipōla oruvaṉ
       ṟāṅkiya perumai" (vīra. poruḷ. 16, urai)

   tāṅkiṉāṟpōlat tampaṭaiyaikkeṭuttu1mikkuvarukiṉṟa pakaivarpaṭaiyai

   :] viyappum - nī avvāciriyariṭattuppeṟṟa kantaḻiyiṉ aticayamum,
   cālpum-piṉpu peṟṟamainta amaitiyum, kaḷaic ceytu ciṟappuṟṟamaiyiṉ
   ippeyar peṟṟāṉ ; "naṟpaṉuvaṉāl vētat,taruñ cīrttip peruṅkaṇṇuṟai,

   viyaṉkaḷam palakol"(puṟanā. 15:17-21) eṉpataṉāl ivaṉ vēḷvi pala
   ceytataṟiyappaṭum."kolyāṉai palavōṭṭik kūṭāmaṉṉar kulantavirtta,
   palyāka mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti yeṉṉumpāṇṭiyātirāja(ṉā)ṉ, nākamā
   malarccōlai naḷirciṉai micai vaṇṭalampum pākaṉūrk kūṟṟameṉṉumpaḻaṉak
   kiṭakkai nīrnāṭṭuc, coṟkaṇṇāḷar colappaṭṭa srī timārkkam piḻaiyāta,
   koṟkaikkiḻāṉ naṟkoṟṟaṉ koṇṭa vēḷvi muṟṟuvikkak,kēḷviyanta ṇāḷar muṉpu
   kēṭkaveṉṟeṭutturaittu,vēḷviccālai muṉpu niṉṟu vēḷvikuṭi yeṉṟappatiyaic,
   cīrōṭu tiruvaḷarac ceytār vēntaṉap poḻutē nīrōṭaṭṭik koṭuttamaiyāṉ"
   (vēḷvikkuṭic cāsaṉam, Epigraphia Indica Vol. 17, No. 16) eṉṟa cāsaṉap
   pakutiyiṉāl ivaṉ vēḷvi ceytārkkut tāṉam pala ceytāṉeṉpatu
   aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṉaip paṟṟiya piṟa ceytikaḷ, puṟanāṉūṟṟup
   patippiluḷḷa pāṭappaṭṭōr varalāṟṟāl aṟiyalākum.
   pukār aṟu ciṟappiṉ cemmaicāṉṟōr palar vāy tōṉṟi-kuṟṟamaṟṟa
   ciṟappukkaḷaiyuṭaiyatalaimaikaḷellām amaintōr palarum

   pakkamum"eṉpuḻik kūṟappaṭṭavai.
